📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期季节性流感病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 携带NA-S247N替代位点的流感甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09病毒的患病率从2024/25流感季的1.2%上升至2025/26流感季的56.8%,到2026年3月为止,这些病毒占分析病毒的最高比例达80%。携带NA-S247N替代位点的病毒表现出比野生型病毒更高的奥司他韦IC50值,且当NA-S247N与223位点的替代位点结合时,耐药性进一步增加,其IC50值最高可达野生型病毒的17倍。
  • 2025年冬季态势评估计划在整个冬季季节以及非季节性甲型H3N2流感暴发期间提供了每周报告。尽管亚型数据有限,分析在整体流感病例下降期间提供了早期信号,表明甲型H3N2流感活动正在增加;甲型H3N2活动的增加最早在7月被检测到,到8月中旬,所有司法管辖区均估计出现增加趋势。
  • 在1255株具有完整八段序列数据的病毒中,共鉴定出168种不同的特征,其中一种特征占主导地位,出现在744株(59.3%)病毒中,而第二常见的特征出现在139株(11.1%);其余特征的频率显著较低。
  • 主要结果包括列出2026-2027流感季节在美国可获得的流感疫苗。未报告具体的数值结果或统计结果。 [10.58347/tml.2026.1765a]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • The prevalence of A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses with the NA-S247N substitution increased from 1.2% in the 2024/25 season to 56.8% in the 2025/26 season, accounting for up to 80% of analyzed viruses by March 2026.
  • The 2025 Winter Situational Assessment Program provided weekly reports throughout the winter season and during the out-of-season influenza A H3N2 epidemic. [10.64898/2026.09.30.26364449].
  • Among 1,255 viruses with complete eight-segment sequence data, 168 distinct signatures were identified, with a single signature predominating in 744 (59.3%) viruses, and the second most frequent signature occurring in 139 (11.1%).
  • Cross-correlation analysis found evidence of correlation between HA-CDI and influenza rates at a lag of zero (R = 0.41, 95% CI 0.08 to 0.65), suggesting a seasonal relationship.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 本期季节性流感病毒新闻集中呈现以下动态。
  • CDC发布了2026年第37周(截至9月19日)的流感监测周报,显示流感活动水平较低。报告包含来自临床和公共卫生实验室的病毒学监测数据。 [news-8768c349730cebfd]。
  • 香港卫生防护中心(CHP)记录了一例严重的儿童流感感染病例。患者于2026年10月2日出现发热、咳嗽、流鼻涕和头晕症状,并于2026年10月4日被送入儿科重症监护病房。卫生防护中心还表示,患者在潜伏期内没有旅行史,并未接种2026/27 季节性流感 疫苗接种(SIV)。
  • 一名来自河内的19个月大的儿童被诊断为RSV引起的急性支气管炎和急性鼻咽炎。该儿童表现出持续发热、咳嗽和呼吸困难,症状发展为喘息、呼吸急促、胸骨凹陷和其他症状。 [news-47c341dbf7b75445]。
  • 一项报告流感甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09病毒中神经氨酸酶替代NA-S247N出现显著增加的研究,并指出其与奥司他韦抗性略有降低相关。该研究已被确认并发表于《国际医学微生物学杂志》。 [news-dd9f8979c20825b3]。
  • This reporting window highlights the following Seasonal influenza viruses news developments.
  • The CDC released a weekly influenza surveillance report for Week 37, ending September 19, 2026, indicating low seasonal influenza activity.
  • A severe paediatric influenza infection case was recorded by the Centre for Health Protection (CHP) in Hong Kong.
  • A 19-month-old child from Hanoi was diagnosed with acute bronchiolitis due to RSV and acute nasopharyngitis.
  • A research study reporting a marked increase in the prevalence of the neuraminidase substitution NA‐S247N in influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses and its associated modest reduction in oseltamivir susceptibility.

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: International Journal of Medical Microbiology  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-10-01  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.ijmm.2026.151745  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 323, 151745
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

德国2024-2026年流感甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09病毒循环增加且对奥司他韦耐药性降低。

Increased circulation of influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses with reduced susceptibility to oseltamivir, Germany 2024-2026.
作者： Susanne C. Duwe, Mohammad Naneh, Jeanette Milde, Ralf Dürrwald, R. Duerrwald
摘要中文翻译背景:奥司他韦仍被认为是严重季节性流感的首选抗病毒药物 治疗。然而,对神经氨酸酶(NA)抑制剂的耐药性给流感管理带来了重大挑战。自2023/24流感季以来,德国、其他欧盟/欧洲经济区国家以及世界卫生组织(WHO)部分地区的流感甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09病毒中出现了携带神经氨酸酶替代位点(NA-I223V和/或NA-S247N)的病毒株,这些病毒株与奥司他韦耐药性降低有关。方法:开发并验证了一种快速RT-PCR/焦磷酸测序检测方法,用于检测NA-I223X和NA-S247X替代位点。随后,通过荧光神经氨酸酶抑制试验和/或下一代测序和焦磷酸测序对德国2024/25和2025/26流感季中检测到的765株甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09病毒进行了表型和基因型特征分析。结果:携带NA-S247N替代位点的甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09病毒的患病率从2024/25流感季的1.2%上升至2025/26流感季的56.8%,到2026年3月为止,这些病毒占分析病毒的最高比例达80%。携带NA-S247N替代位点的病毒表现出比野生型病毒更高的奥司他韦IC50值。当NA-S247N与223位点的替代位点结合时,耐药性进一步增加,其IC50值最高可达野生型病毒的17倍。结论:流感甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09病毒中神经氨酸酶替代位点的积累,特别是NA-S247N与NA-I223V/T的组合,显著降低了奥司他韦的敏感性。NA-S247N的显著增加表明该替代位点正在变得持续存在而非暂时现象。靶向焦磷酸测序是一种实用工具,可用于早期检测与耐药性相关的新兴替代位点。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在调查德国流感甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09病毒对奥司他韦耐药性的出现和传播情况,因为对神经氨酸酶抑制剂的耐药性给流感管理带来了重大挑战。
设计与对象
本研究对德国2024/25和2025/26流感季中检测到的流感甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09病毒进行了特征分析,共分析了765株病毒以评估其对奥司他韦的敏感性。
核心方法
开发并验证了一种快速RT-PCR/焦磷酸测序检测方法,用于检测NA-I223X和NA-S247X替代位点,并通过荧光神经氨酸酶抑制试验进行表型分析,同时通过下一代测序和焦磷酸测序进行基因型分析。
主要结果
携带NA-S247N替代位点的流感甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09病毒的患病率从2024/25流感季的1.2%上升至2025/26流感季的56.8%,到2026年3月为止,这些病毒占分析病毒的最高比例达80%。携带NA-S247N替代位点的病毒表现出比野生型病毒更高的奥司他韦IC50值,且当NA-S247N与223位点的替代位点结合时,耐药性进一步增加,其IC50值最高可达野生型病毒的17倍。
解释与创新
作者认为,神经氨酸酶替代位点的积累,特别是NA-S247N与NA-I223V/T的组合,显著降低了奥司他韦的敏感性,表明循环中的流感甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09病毒的遗传特征正在发生变化。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现强调了需要更新监测和诊断方法,以检测流感甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09病毒中与耐药性相关的新兴替代位点,这可能有助于制定治疗策略和公共卫生应对措施。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其依赖于表型和基因型特征分析方法,且由于可能存在未测量的混杂因素以及仅限于单一地理区域,证据强度中等。

Increased circulation of influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses with reduced susceptibility to oseltamivir, Germany 2024-2026.

Authors: Susanne C. Duwe, Mohammad Naneh, Jeanette Milde, Ralf Dürrwald, R. Duerrwald
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Oseltamivir is still considered to be the first-line antiviral treatment for severe seasonal influenza. However, resistance to neuraminidase (NA) inhibitors poses significant challenges to influenza management. Since the 2023/24 season, A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses carrying neuraminidase substitutions (NA-I223V and/or NA-S247N) have emerged in Germany, other EU/EEA countries, and some of the World Health Organization (WHO) regions. These viruses have been linked to reduced oseltamivir susceptibility. METHODS: A rapid RT-PCR/pyrosequencing assay was developed and validated to detect NA-I223X and NA-S247X substitutions. Subsequently, a total of 765 A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses detected in Germany during the 2024/25 and 2025/26 influenza seasons were characterized phenotypically using fluorometric NA inhibition testing and/or genotypically using next-generation sequencing and pyrosequencing. RESULTS: The prevalence of A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses bearing the NA-S247N substitution increased from 1.2% in the 2024/25 season to 56.8% in the 2025/26 season, accounting for up to 80% of analyzed viruses by March 2026. Viruses carrying NA-S247N exhibited higher oseltamivir IC50 values than wild-type viruses. Combinations of NA-S247N with substitutions at position 223 demonstrated an additional increase. In viruses carrying the combined NA-S247N and NA-I223V/T substitutions oseltamivir showed reduced inhibition with increased IC50 values of up to 17-fold compared to wild type viruses. CONCLUSIONS: The accumulation of NA substitutions in A(H1N1)pdm09 influenza viruses, particularly NA-S247N in combination with NA-I223V/T, substantially reduces susceptibility to oseltamivir. The marked increase in NA-S247N prevalence suggests that this substitution is becoming persistent rather than transient. Targeted pyrosequencing is a practical tool for the early detection of emerging resistance-associated substitutions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the emergence and spread of influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses with reduced susceptibility to oseltamivir in Germany, as resistance to neuraminidase inhibitors poses significant challenges to influenza management.
Design and population
The study characterized A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses detected in Germany during the 2024/25 and 2025/26 influenza seasons, analyzing a total of 765 viruses to assess their susceptibility to oseltamivir.
Core methods
A rapid RT-PCR/pyrosequencing assay was developed and validated to detect NA-I223X and NA-S247X substitutions, and phenotypic characterization was performed using fluorometric NA inhibition testing alongside genotypic analysis via next-generation sequencing and pyrosequencing.
Main results
The prevalence of A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses with the NA-S247N substitution increased from 1.2% in the 2024/25 season to 56.8% in the 2025/26 season, accounting for up to 80% of analyzed viruses by March 2026. Viruses with NA-S247N had higher oseltamivir IC50 values than wild-type viruses, and combinations of NA-S247N with substitutions at position 223 showed further increased resistance, with IC50 values up to 17-fold higher than wild-type viruses.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the accumulation of NA substitutions, particularly NA-S247N in combination with NA-I223V/T, significantly reduces oseltamivir susceptibility, indicating a shift in the genetic profile of circulating A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for updated surveillance and diagnostic methods to detect emerging resistance-associated substitutions in A(H1N1)pdm09 viruses, which could inform treatment strategies and public health responses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on phenotypic and genotypic characterization methods, and the evidence is moderate due to the potential for unmeasured confounding factors and the focus on a single geographic region.

猪流感A病毒在越南的分子进化和传播特征,2020-2024年。

Molecular Epidemiology and Evolution of Swine Influenza A Viruses, Vietnam, 2020-2024.
作者： Ngoc Anh Bui, Zoe Yi Song, Samuel M. S. Cheng, Duy Tung Dao, Lydia D J Chang, Gigi Y. Z. Liu, Quang Huy Nguyen, Thi Hang Dang, Tom L. Y. Woo, Coco H. C. Chan
摘要中文翻译猪流感A病毒(IAV-S)引发了2009年的H1N1大流行,并对未来的动物源性和大流行威胁构成潜在风险。越南是东亚和东南亚地区IAV-S出现的关键热点,拥有密集的猪和人类人口以及活跃的畜牧业贸易。我们对2020-2024年期间越南的IAV-S进行了分子分析,扩展了此前2013-2019年的研究。通过分析56株IAV-S分离株(21株H1N1、31株H1N2和4株H3N2),我们鉴定了多个共循环的H1和H3分支,包括大流行H1N1、欧亚禽源样和欧洲谱系。三个H1分支仅在越南持续存在,循环时间长达12年。系统发育地理分析揭示了来自北美、欧洲、中国、泰国和柬埔寨的多次独立引入事件。我们检测到广泛的基因重配,经常涉及大流行H1N1病毒的内部基因。我们还鉴定了与哺乳动物适应相关的多个谱系特异性特征。研究结果强调了IAV-S在越南持续存在的传播特征,并突显了需要持续监测的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解猪流感A病毒(IAV-S)在越南的分子进化和传播特征,应对IAV-S作为动物源性和大流行风险的持续威胁。越南因其密集的猪和人类人口以及活跃的畜牧业贸易,是IAV-S出现的关键热点。
设计与对象
本研究涉及对2020年至2024年期间越南的IAV-S进行分子分析,扩展了此前2013年至2019年的研究。研究覆盖了多个共循环的H1和H3分支,包括大流行H1N1、欧亚禽源样和欧洲谱系,共分析了56株IAV-S分离株(21株H1N1、31株H1N2和4株H3N2)。
核心方法
分子分析通过分析56株IAV-S分离株的系统发育和系统发育地理特征进行。研究鉴定了多个共循环的分支,并检测到涉及大流行H1N1病毒内部基因的广泛基因重配。还鉴定了与哺乳动物适应相关的多个谱系特异性特征。
主要结果
研究鉴定了多个共循环的H1和H3分支,包括大流行H1N1、欧亚禽源样和欧洲谱系,来自56株IAV-S分离株。系统发育地理分析揭示了来自北美、欧洲、中国、泰国和柬埔寨的多次独立引入事件。检测到涉及大流行H1N1病毒内部基因的广泛基因重配,并鉴定了与哺乳动物适应相关的多个谱系特异性特征。
解释与创新
研究结果强调了IAV-S在越南持续传播的特征,突显了需要持续监测的重要性。识别了新的特征和基因重配事件,为病毒适应哺乳动物宿主提供了见解。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果对监测、基因组监测以及理解未来动物源性和大流行威胁的潜力具有重要意义。它们强调了在越南及其他猪和人类人口密集地区持续监测IAV-S的重要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性包括可能存在抽样偏差,以及需要进一步研究以了解所识别特征和基因重配事件的全部影响。由于研究的观察性质和缺乏纵向数据,证据强度中等,需进一步确认研究结果的重要性。

Molecular Epidemiology and Evolution of Swine Influenza A Viruses, Vietnam, 2020-2024.

Authors: Ngoc Anh Bui, Zoe Yi Song, Samuel M. S. Cheng, Duy Tung Dao, Lydia D J Chang, Gigi Y. Z. Liu, Quang Huy Nguyen, Thi Hang Dang, Tom L. Y. Woo, Coco H. C. Chan
Original abstract (English)
Swine influenza A viruses (IAV-S) caused the 2009 H1N1 pandemic and pose a future zoonotic and pandemic threat. Vietnam represents a critical hotspot for IAV-S emergence within East and Southeast Asia, with dense swine and human populations and intensive livestock trade. We conducted genomic surveillance of IAV-S in Vietnam during 2020-2024, extending previous surveillance from 2013-2019. We identified multiple co-circulating H1 and H3 clades, including pandemic H1N1, Eurasian avian-like, and European lineages, by conducting phylogenetic analysis of 56 IAV-S isolates (21 H1N1, 31 H1N2, and 4 H3N2). Three H1 clades persisted exclusively in Vietnam, circulating up to 12 years. Phylogeographic analysis revealed multiple independent introduction events from North America, Europe, China, Thailand, and Cambodia. We detected extensive reassortment that frequently involved pandemic H1N1 virus internal genes. We identified several lineage-specific mutations associated with mammalian adaptation. Our findings underscore the ongoing IAV-S evolution and need for sustained surveillance in Vietnam.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the molecular epidemiology and evolution of swine influenza A viruses (IAV-S) in Vietnam, addressing the ongoing threat of IAV-S as a zoonotic and pandemic risk. Vietnam, with its dense swine and human populations and intensive livestock trade, is a critical hotspot for IAV-S emergence.
Design and population
The study involved genomic surveillance of IAV-S in Vietnam from 2020 to 2024, extending previous surveillance from 2013 to 2019. The research covered multiple co-circulating H1 and H3 clades, including pandemic H1N1, Eurasian avian-like, and European lineages, from 56 IAV-S isolates (21 H1N1, 31 H1N2, and 4 H3N2).
Core methods
Genomic surveillance was conducted by phylogenetic and phylogeographic analysis of 56 IAV-S isolates. The study identified multiple co-circulating clades and extensive reassortment involving pandemic H1N1 virus internal genes. Lineage-specific mutations associated with mammalian adaptation were also identified.
Main results
The study identified multiple co-circulating H1 and H3 clades, including pandemic H1N1, Eurasian avian-like, and European lineages, from 56 IAV-S isolates. Phylogeographic analysis revealed multiple independent introduction events from North America, Europe, China, Thailand, and Cambodia. Extensive reassortment involving pandemic H1N1 virus internal genes was detected, and several lineage-specific mutations associated with mammalian adaptation were identified.
Interpretation and novelty
The study's findings underscore the ongoing evolution of IAV-S in Vietnam, highlighting the need for sustained surveillance. Novel mutations and reassortment events were identified, providing insights into the virus's adaptation to mammalian hosts.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings have implications for surveillance, diagnosis, and understanding the potential for future zoonotic and pandemic threats. They emphasize the importance of continued monitoring of IAV-S in Vietnam and other regions with dense swine and human populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the potential for sampling bias and the need for further research to understand the full impact of identified mutations and reassortment events. The evidence strength is moderate due to the observational nature of the study and the need for longitudinal data to confirm the significance of the findings.

婴儿初次流感感染或季节性流感暴露后抗体反应的纵向差异:一项前瞻性出生队列研究

Distinct Longitudinal Antibody Responses Following Primary Influenza Infection or Vaccination in Infants: A Prospective Birth Cohort Study
作者： Devyani J. Joshi, Sanjeev Kumar, Veronika I. Zarnitsyna, Allison R Burrell, Chelsea Rolhfs, Shamika Danzy, Tysheena Perkins Charles, Faith A. Mbadugha, Ling-Ling Xu, Daniel C. Payne
摘要中文翻译初次流感暴露对婴儿期抗体反应的强度、持续时间和抗原特异性所产生的纵向影响仍不完全清楚。我们在一项前瞻性美国出生队列研究中调查了婴儿在初次流感感染或季节性流感暴露后体液抗体反应的情况,该研究每周对流感感染进行呼吸道监测。纵向评估了针对H1和H3血凝素(HA)抗原的流感特异性血清IgG结合反应、血凝素抑制(HAI)反应以及活病毒中和抗体反应。为减少母源抗体的影响,初次暴露反应的分析仅限于那些在暴露前流感特异性母源抗体已降至检测限以下的婴儿。比较了初次感染和流感暴露后的抗体反应,并在随后的年度流感暴露后进行了检查。我们发现,母源流感特异性IgG在婴儿早期迅速下降。在那些暴露前未检测到流感特异性抗体的婴儿中,初次流感感染与暴露后的抗体反应强度更大,且在随访期间下降更不明显,而初次流感暴露则表现出不同的情况。这些群体在年龄、日历季节和暴露特征上存在差异,限制了将这些差异直接归因于暴露途径。对于先前感染过流感病毒后又经历流感暴露的婴儿,在随后的流感暴露中,其抗体反应最初对之前感染的亚型表现出更强的结合能力。随着重复的流感暴露,抗体反应逐渐分布到所测试的抗原中。我们的研究结果揭示了根据流感暴露的性质和顺序,抗体反应的强度、持续时间和抗原特异性存在差异。通过前瞻性地记录生命早期的暴露情况,本研究提供了将暴露史与随后体液抗体反应联系起来的纵向证据。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
初次流感暴露对婴儿期抗体反应的强度、持续时间和抗原特异性所产生的纵向影响仍不完全清楚。
设计与对象
我们在一项前瞻性美国出生队列研究中调查了婴儿在初次流感感染或季节性流感暴露后体液抗体反应的情况,该研究每周对流感感染进行呼吸道监测。
核心方法
纵向评估了针对H1和H3血凝素(HA)抗原的流感特异性血清IgG结合反应、血凝素抑制(HAI)反应以及活病毒中和抗体反应。
主要结果
我们发现,母源流感特异性IgG在婴儿早期迅速下降。
解释与创新
比较了初次感染和流感暴露后的抗体反应,并在随后的年度流感暴露后进行了检查。
科研与公卫意义
我们的研究结果揭示了根据流感暴露的性质和顺序,抗体反应的强度、持续时间和抗原特异性存在差异。
局限与证据强度
这些群体在年龄、日历季节和暴露特征上存在差异,限制了将这些差异直接归因于暴露途径。

Distinct Longitudinal Antibody Responses Following Primary Influenza Infection or Vaccination in Infants: A Prospective Birth Cohort Study

Authors: Devyani J. Joshi, Sanjeev Kumar, Veronika I. Zarnitsyna, Allison R Burrell, Chelsea Rolhfs, Shamika Danzy, Tysheena Perkins Charles, Faith A. Mbadugha, Ling-Ling Xu, Daniel C. Payne
Original abstract (English)
The longitudinal effects of initial influenza exposure on the magnitude, persistence, and antigen specificity of antibody responses during infancy remain incompletely understood. We investigated humoral antibody responses following primary influenza infection or seasonal vaccination in a prospective US birth cohort with weekly respiratory surveillance for influenza infection. Influenza-specific serum IgG binding to H1 and H3 haemagglutinin (HA) antigens, HA inhibition (HAI), and live-virus neutralizing antibody responses were assessed longitudinally. To minimize the influence of maternally derived antibodies, analyses of primary exposure responses were restricted to infants for whom influenza-specific maternal antibodies had declined below the assay detection limit before exposure. Antibody responses were compared following primary infection and vaccination and examined after subsequent annual vaccination. We found that maternal influenza-specific IgG declined rapidly during early infancy. Among infants without detectable pre-exposure influenza-specific antibodies, primary influenza infection was associated with greater post-exposure antibody responses and less measurable decline over follow-up than primary vaccination. These groups differed in age, calendar season, and exposure characteristics, limiting direct attribution of these differences to the route of exposure. In infants with previous influenza infection followed by vaccination, responses after subsequent vaccination initially showed greater binding to the previously infecting subtype among the tested HA antigens. With repeated vaccination, responses became more distributed across the tested antigens. Our findings reveal differences in the magnitude, persistence, and antigen specificity of antibody responses according to the nature and sequence of influenza exposure. By prospectively capturing early-life exposures, this study provides longitudinal evidence linking exposure history to subsequent humoral antibody responses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The longitudinal effects of initial influenza exposure on the magnitude, persistence, and antigen specificity of antibody responses during infancy remain incompletely understood.
Design and population
We investigated humoral antibody responses following primary influenza infection or seasonal vaccination in a prospective US birth cohort with weekly respiratory surveillance for influenza infection.
Core methods
Influenza-specific serum IgG binding to H1 and H3 haemagglutinin (HA) antigens, HA inhibition (HAI), and live-virus neutralizing antibody responses were assessed longitudinally.
Main results
We found that maternal influenza-specific IgG declined rapidly during early infancy.
Interpretation and novelty
Antibody responses were compared following primary infection and vaccination and examined after subsequent annual vaccination.
Scientific and public-health significance
Our findings reveal differences in the magnitude, persistence, and antigen specificity of antibody responses according to the nature and sequence of influenza exposure.
Limitations and evidence strength
These groups differed in age, calendar season, and exposure characteristics, limiting direct attribution of these differences to the route of exposure.

季节性流感 疫苗接种: 2026-2027

Seasonal Influenza Vaccination: 2026-2027
作者： A. Ropero, Nathalie El Omeiri, B. Jauregui
摘要中文翻译所有≥6个月的无禁忌症人群均建议每年接种一次流感疫苗接种。1-6 本季在美国可获得的流感疫苗列于表3中。

问题与背景
所有≥6个月的无禁忌症人群均建议每年接种一次流感疫苗接种。1-6 本研究旨在解决基于当前季节流感病毒的更新疫苗接种指南的需求。
设计与对象
本研究是对2026-2027流感季节的回顾,重点关注美国的季节性流感病毒。研究对象包括所有≥6个月且无疫苗接种禁忌症的人群。

主要结果
主要结果包括列出2026-2027流感季节在美国可获得的流感疫苗。未报告具体的数值结果或统计结果。

研究结果为季节性流感 疫苗接种的公共卫生建议提供了依据,并强调了更新疫苗配方对于有效预防的重要性。

Seasonal Influenza Vaccination: 2026-2027

Authors: A. Ropero, Nathalie El Omeiri, B. Jauregui
Original abstract (English)
Annual influenza vaccination is recommended for all persons ≥6 months old without a contraindication.1-6 Influenza vaccines available this season in the US are listed in Table 3.

Question and background
Annual influenza vaccination is recommended for all persons ≥6 months old without a contraindication.1-6 The study addresses the need for updated vaccination guidelines based on the current season's influenza viruses.
Design and population
The study is a review of the 2026-2027 influenza season, focusing on seasonal influenza viruses in the US. It includes all persons ≥6 months old without contraindications to vaccination.
Core methods
The methods involve reviewing influenza vaccines available this season in the US, as listed in Table 3. The study does not include detailed statistical or laboratory procedures.
Main results
The main results include the listing of influenza vaccines available for the 2026-2027 season in the US. No specific numerical findings or statistical results are reported.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that annual influenza vaccination remains a critical public health measure. The novelty lies in the updated vaccine recommendations for the current season.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform public health recommendations for seasonal influenza vaccination and highlight the importance of updated vaccine formulations for effective prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on a review of available vaccines and does not include detailed data on vaccine efficacy or outcomes. The evidence is moderate as it relies on existing guidelines rather than new empirical data.

澳大利亚2025年冬季呼吸道病毒流行态势实时评估及非季节性甲型H3N2流感暴发

Real-time situational assessment of respiratory virus epidemics in Australia over winter 2025 and an out-of-season influenza A H3N2 epidemic
作者： Oliver D. Eales, Alys M. Young, Katharine L. Senior, Ruarai J. Tobin, Janaki Amin, Brett Nicholas Archer, Paul Armstrong, Sally Ellis, Elizabeth J. Robinson, Janet Strachan
摘要中文翻译引言:在温带地区,流感和呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)通常在冬季表现出季节性流行,常与SARS-CoV-2流行同时发生。监测过去和实时的感染动态趋势对于提升公共卫生态势感知能力至关重要,包括识别和描述异常的流行活动。然而,用于监测这些趋势的数据可能包含大量噪声,包括报告中的强周日效应。此外,如果未结合病毒学数据,流感病例数据可能会掩盖流感亚型中的不同趋势。目标:通过使用统计方法量化呼吸道病毒活动趋势,澳大利亚-新西兰流行预测与分析联盟(ACEFA)旨在为政府合作伙伴提供全面的流行病学情报。方法:分析了澳大利亚八个司法管辖区每日的SARS-CoV-2、RSV和流感病例时间序列数据。使用贝叶斯p样条模型对每种病原体和每种流感亚型的过去和实时趋势进行了推断。结果:2025年冬季态势评估计划在整个冬季季节以及非季节性甲型H3N2流感暴发期间提供了每周报告,这一暴发由新型流感亚群('亚群K')的出现所驱动。尽管亚型数据有限,我们的分析在整体流感病例下降期间提供了早期信号,表明甲型H3N2流感活动正在增加;甲型H3N2活动的增加最早在7月被检测到,到8月中旬,所有司法管辖区均估计出现增加趋势。讨论:我们的分析突显了在实时流行态势评估中整合统计方法以及在各司法管辖区整合报告的益处。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在监测过去和实时的呼吸道病毒活动趋势,特别是流感甲型H3N2流感病毒,以在2025年冬季季节和非季节性甲型H3N2流感暴发期间提升公共卫生态势感知能力。在温带地区,流感和呼吸道合胞病毒通常在冬季表现出季节性流行,常与SARS-CoV-2流行同时发生,用于监测这些趋势的数据可能包含大量噪声,包括报告中的强周日效应。
设计与对象
本研究分析了澳大利亚八个司法管辖区每日的SARS-CoV-2、RSV和流感病例时间序列数据。使用贝叶斯p样条模型对每种病原体和每种流感亚型的过去和实时趋势进行了推断。
核心方法
分析了澳大利亚八个司法管辖区每日的SARS-CoV-2、RSV和流感病例时间序列数据。
主要结果
2025年冬季态势评估计划在整个冬季季节以及非季节性甲型H3N2流感暴发期间提供了每周报告。尽管亚型数据有限,分析在整体流感病例下降期间提供了早期信号,表明甲型H3N2流感活动正在增加;甲型H3N2活动的增加最早在7月被检测到,到8月中旬,所有司法管辖区均估计出现增加趋势。
解释与创新
我们的分析突显了在实时流行态势评估中整合统计方法以及在各司法管辖区整合报告的益处。利用贝叶斯p样条模型早期检测到甲型H3N2流感活动的增加,为公共卫生行动提供了重要信号。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为监测、诊断以及应对流感甲型H3N2流感病毒暴发的公共卫生措施提供信息。早期检测到活动增加有助于及时干预以减轻疫情的影响。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性包括使用有限的亚型数据以及报告中可能存在的周日效应。由于这些局限性,证据强度中等。

Real-time situational assessment of respiratory virus epidemics in Australia over winter 2025 and an out-of-season influenza A H3N2 epidemic

Authors: Oliver D. Eales, Alys M. Young, Katharine L. Senior, Ruarai J. Tobin, Janaki Amin, Brett Nicholas Archer, Paul Armstrong, Sally Ellis, Elizabeth J. Robinson, Janet Strachan
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: In temperate regions, influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) typically exhibit seasonal epidemics during the winter months, often concurrently with SARS-CoV-2 epidemics. Monitoring past and real-time trends in infection dynamics is important for informing public health situational awareness, including identifying and characterising unusual epidemic activity. However, data used to monitor these trends can exhibit substantial noise including strong day-of-the-week variation in reporting. In addition, case data for influenza can obscure distinct trends in influenza subtypes if not combined with virological data. Aim: By quantifying trends in respiratory virus activity using statistical methods, the Australia–Aotearoa Consortium for Epidemic Forecasting and Analytics (ACEFA) aims to provide government partners with comprehensive epidemic intelligence. Methods: Analyses were performed on daily case time series data for SARS-CoV-2, RSV and influenza across Australia's eight jurisdictions. Past and real-time trends were inferred for each pathogen and each influenza subtype using Bayesian p-spline models. Results: The 2025 Winter Situational Assessment Program provided weekly reports throughout the winter season and during the out-of-season influenza A H3N2 epidemic, driven by the emergence of the novel influenza subclade ('subclade K'). Despite limited subtyping data, our analysis provided an early signal of increasing influenza A H3N2 during a period in which overall influenza cases were decreasing; increases in influenza A H3N2 activity were first detected in July, and by mid-August, increasing H3N2 activity was estimated across all jurisdictions. Discussion: Our analysis highlights the benefits of incorporating statistical methods into real-time epidemic situational assessment and in consolidating reporting across jurisdictions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to monitor past and real-time trends in respiratory virus activity, particularly influenza A(H3N2), to inform public health situational awareness during the 2025 winter season and an out-of-season influenza A H3N2 epidemic. In temperate regions, influenza and respiratory syncytial virus typically exhibit seasonal epidemics during winter, often concurrently with SARS-CoV-2 epidemics, and data used to monitor these trends can exhibit substantial noise including strong day-of-the-week variation in reporting.
Design and population
The study involved analyses of daily case time series data for SARS-CoV-2, RSV, and influenza across Australia's eight jurisdictions. Past and real-time trends were inferred for each pathogen and each influenza subtype using Bayesian p-spline models.
Core methods
Analyses were performed on daily case time series data for SARS-CoV-2, RSV, and influenza across Australia's eight jurisdictions.
Main results
The 2025 Winter Situational Assessment Program provided weekly reports throughout the winter season and during the out-of-season influenza A H3N2 epidemic. Despite limited subtyping data, the analysis provided an early signal of increasing influenza A H3N2 during a period in which overall influenza cases were decreasing; increases in influenza A H3N2 activity were first detected in July, and by mid-August, increasing H3N2 activity was estimated across all jurisdictions.
Interpretation and novelty
The analysis highlights the benefits of incorporating statistical methods into real-time epidemic situational assessment and in consolidating reporting across jurisdictions. The early detection of increasing influenza A H3N2 activity using Bayesian p-spline models provided an important signal for public health action.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and public health responses to influenza A(H3N2) epidemics. The early detection of increasing activity can help in timely interventions to mitigate the impact of the epidemic.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the use of limited subtyping data and the potential for day-of-the-week variation in reporting. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.

E蛋白驱动的iNKT亚群调控塑造了流感A病毒感染早期的免疫应答.

E protein-driven iNKT subset modulation shapes early immune responses during influenza A virus infection.
作者： Luczak JA, Milek O, Carter H, Schafer CM, Ainsua-Enrich E, Griffin CT, Walters MS, Kovats S, Alberola-Ila J
摘要中文翻译不变的自然杀伤T细胞(iNKT细胞)在流感A病毒(IAV)感染的早期阶段通过影响炎症和免疫调节发挥关键作用,但不同iNKT功能亚群(iNKT1、iNKT2和iNKT17)对这些反应的影响尚不清楚。我们使用了具有正常iNKT细胞数量但不同iNKT亚群比例的基因改造小鼠品系(NKTWT和NKTET2)来分析不同iNKT功能亚群对IAV感染结果的影响。我们发现,感染IAV的NKTET2小鼠表现出体重减轻减少、髓系细胞招募和激活减弱,以及与对照组相比肺部感染区域减少了40%。这些变化伴随着炎症介质(Ifna、Isg15、Ifit1)和趋化因子Ccl2和Cxcl2的表达降低,以及III型干扰素(Ifnl3)水平的升高。对iNKT细胞的单细胞RNA测序分析表明,这些变化是由iNKT反应的定量差异驱动的,其中NKTWT小鼠的iNKT反应主要为I型,而NKTET2小鼠的iNKT反应则主要为17型。这些差异与NKTET2小鼠肺部Il22b和Il1b水平的升高相关。总体而言,我们的结果表明,iNKT亚群比例的变化通过改变早期免疫应答的特征来影响IAV感染的结果,并提示未来可以选择性激活iNKT亚群以改善疫苗接种策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在探讨不同iNKT功能亚群对流感A病毒(IAV)感染早期免疫应答的影响,因为iNKT细胞已知会影响炎症和免疫调节,但它们在IAV感染中的具体作用仍不清楚。
设计与对象
本研究使用了具有正常iNKT细胞数量但不同亚群比例的基因改造小鼠品系(NKTWT和NKTET2)来分析这些亚群对IAV感染结果的影响。
核心方法
采用单细胞RNA测序分析iNKT反应,揭示这些变化是由iNKT反应的定量差异驱动的,其中NKTWT小鼠的iNKT反应主要为I型,而NKTET2小鼠的iNKT反应则主要为17型。
主要结果
感染IAV的NKTET2小鼠表现出体重减轻减少、髓系细胞招募和激活减弱,以及与对照组相比肺部感染区域减少了40%。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,iNKT亚群比例的变化通过改变早期免疫应答的特征显著影响IAV感染的结果。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果突显了选择性激活iNKT亚群在改善针对季节性流感病毒的疫苗接种策略方面的潜力。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅限于小鼠模型,可能无法完全适用于人类结果,且证据基于单一对照组,表明研究结果的可信度中等。

E protein-driven iNKT subset modulation shapes early immune responses during influenza A virus infection.

Authors: Luczak JA, Milek O, Carter H, Schafer CM, Ainsua-Enrich E, Griffin CT, Walters MS, Kovats S, Alberola-Ila J
Original abstract (English)
Invariant natural killer T (iNKT) cells play a critical role in the early phases of the response to influenza A virus (IAV) infection by influencing inflammation and immune regulation, but the impact of the different iNKT functional subsets (iNKT1, iNKT2, and iNKT17) in these responses is unclear. We used genetically altered mouse strains with normal numbers of iNKT cells but different iNKT subset representation (NKTWT and NKTET2) to analyze the impact of different iNKT functional subsets on IAV infection outcomes. We show that IAV-infected NKTET2 mice have reduced weight loss, diminished myeloid recruitment and activation, and a 40% reduction in lung-infected areas compared with controls. This was accompanied by lower expression of inflammatory mediators (Ifna, Isg15, Ifit1) and chemoattractants Ccl2 and Cxcl2, along with elevated levels of type III interferon (Ifnl3). Single-cell RNA sequencing analysis of iNKTs suggests that these changes are driven by quantitative differences in iNKT responses, which are predominantly type I in NKTWT mice but type 17 in NKTET2 mice. These differences correlate with higher levels of Il22b and Il1b in NKTET2 mice lungs. Altogether, our results indicate that changes in iNKT subset representation impact the outcome of IAV infections by changing the character of the early immune response, and suggests that selective activation of iNKT subsets could be used to improve vaccination strategies in the future.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the impact of different iNKT functional subsets on the early immune response to influenza A virus (IAV) infection, as iNKT cells are known to influence inflammation and immune regulation, but their specific roles in IAV infection remain unclear.
Design and population
The study used genetically altered mouse strains (NKTWT and NKTET2) with normal iNKT cell numbers but different subset representation to analyze the effects of these subsets on IAV infection outcomes.
Core methods
Single-cell RNA sequencing was employed to analyze iNKT responses, revealing that these changes are driven by quantitative differences in iNKT responses, which are predominantly type I in NKTWT mice but type 17 in NKTET2 mice.
Main results
IAV-infected NKTET2 mice exhibited reduced weight loss, diminished myeloid recruitment and activation, and a 40% reduction in lung-infected areas compared with controls.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that changes in iNKT subset representation significantly affect the outcome of IAV infections by altering the character of the early immune response.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the potential of selectively activating iNKT subsets to improve vaccination strategies against seasonal influenza viruses.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to mouse models, which may not fully translate to human outcomes, and the evidence is based on a single comparison group, suggesting moderate confidence in the findings.

A-099 为监测和诊断开发人类和禽流感分析参考材料

A-099 Development of Human and Avian Influenza Analytical Reference Materials for Surveillance and Diagnostics
作者： Leka Papazisi, Holly Asbury, Jason Bose, Victoria Victoria Knight-Connoni
摘要中文翻译摘要 为监测和诊断开发人类和禽流感分析参考材料 Holly Ann Asbury,BS;Jason Bose;Victoria Knight-Connoni,PhD;Leka Papazisi,DVM,PhD 背景 人类和高致病性禽流感(HPAI)病毒对公共卫生构成重大威胁,可能引发广泛疾病和经济损失。早期检测和控制暴发依赖于有效的监测和诊断测试。方法 ATCC®为高致病性禽流感病毒血清型H5N1、H5N6、H7N7、H7N9和H9N2;人类流感A病毒血清型H1N1、H3N2和2009年大流行H1N1;以及流感B病毒株开发了一套全面的定量合成分析参考材料(ARMs)。每种合成ARM包含来自第4、5、6、7和8段的完整序列,包括HA、NP、NA、M1、M2、NS1和NEP/NS1基因,覆盖了50%的流感基因组。这些片段是分子检测测试的关键诊断目标,并提供了足够的基因组背景以评估检测特异性。这些ARMs使用高度可靠的合成生物学技术制造,通过下一代测序进行验证,并通过基于数字的PCR进行量化。此外,这些材料不含任何活性成分,可在BSL-1环境下处理。因此,它们旨在作为监测和诊断分子测试的安全且可靠的阳性对照。结果 这些合成ARMs通过多种已发表的定量PCR(qPCR)检测方法进行了实验评估,包括来自疾病控制与预防中心(CDC)、世界卫生组织(WHO)和世界动物卫生组织(WOAH)的方法。此外,禽流感ARMs被证实与亚型特异性环介导等温扩增(LAMP)检测方法以及BioFire Diagnostics FilmArray(bioMérieux)体外诊断设备兼容。我们还使用超过250种已发表的检测方法进行了ARMs兼容性的计算机模拟评估。合成产品在所有测试中均表现与基因组RNA相当。结论 数据表明,所有用于禽流感和人类流感的合成ARMs都设计良好,适合用于开发和验证分子检测和量化检测方法。此外,我们的研究结果表明,合成RNA ARMs与相应的基因组RNA等效,使其成为BSL-3衍生材料的有价值的BSL-1替代品。我们的结果表明,这些合成ARMs可以作为诊断和监测中使用的分子检测方法的可靠和安全的对照。

问题与背景
人类和高致病性禽流感(HPAI)病毒对公共卫生构成重大威胁,可能引发广泛疾病和经济损失。早期检测和控制暴发依赖于有效的监测和诊断测试。

解释与创新
数据表明,所有用于禽流感和人类流感的合成ARMs都设计良好,适合用于开发和验证分子检测和量化检测方法。我们的研究结果表明,合成RNA ARMs与相应的基因组RNA等效,使其成为BSL-3衍生材料的有价值的BSL-1替代品。
科研与公卫意义
这些合成ARMs可以作为诊断和监测中使用的分子检测方法的可靠和安全的对照。它们提供了BSL-3衍生材料的BSL-1替代方案,提高了诊断检测的安全性和可及性。

A-099 Development of Human and Avian Influenza Analytical Reference Materials for Surveillance and Diagnostics

Authors: Leka Papazisi, Holly Asbury, Jason Bose, Victoria Victoria Knight-Connoni
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Abstract Development of Human and Avian Influenza Analytical Reference Materials for Surveillance and Diagnostics Holly Ann Asbury, BS; Jason Bose; Victoria Knight-Connoni, PhD; Leka Papazisi, DVM, PhD Background Human and highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) viruses pose a significant public health threat, with the potential to cause widespread illness and economic losses. Early detection and control of outbreaks depend on effective surveillance and diagnostic testing. Method ATCC® developed a comprehensive suite of quantitative synthetic analytical reference materials (ARMs) for HPAI virus serotypes H5N1, H5N6, H7N7, H7N9, and H9N2; human influenza A virus serotypes H1N1, H3N2, and H1N1 2009 pandemic; and Influenza B virus strains. Each synthetic ARM contains the complete sequences from segments 4, 5, 6, 7, and 8, including the HA, NP, NA, M1, M2, NS1, and NEP/NS1 genes, covering 50% of the influenza genome. These segments are key diagnostic targets for molecular tests and provide sufficient genomic context for assessing assay specificity. These ARMs are manufactured using a highly reliable synthetic biology technology, verified by next-generation sequencing, and quantified via digital-based PCR. Further, they do not contain any viable material and can be handled in BSL-1 settings. As such, they are intended to serve as safe and reliable positive controls for molecular tests for surveillance and diagnostics. Results These synthetic ARMs were experimentally evaluated using several published quantitative PCR (qPCR) assays, including those from the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC), the World Health Organization (WHO), and the World Organization for Animal Health (WOAH). Additionally, the avian influenza ARMs were confirmed to be compatible with subtype-specific loop-mediated isothermal amplification (LAMP) assays and the BioFire Diagnostics FilmArray (bioMérieux) in vitro diagnostic device. We conducted an in silico assessment of ARM compatibility using over 250 published assays. The synthetic products performed as well as genomic RNA in all tests. Conclusions The data show that all synthetic ARMs for avian and human influenza are effectively designed and suitable for developing and validating molecular detection and quantification assays. Moreover, our findings indicate that the synthetic RNA ARMs are equivalent to their corresponding genomic RNA, making them a valuable BSL-1 alternative to BSL-3–derived materials. Our results suggest that these synthetic ARMs can serve as reliable and safe controls for molecular assays used in diagnostics and surveillance.

These ARMs are intended to serve as safe and reliable positive controls for molecular tests for surveillance and diagnostics.
Core methods

These segments are key diagnostic targets for molecular tests and provide sufficient genomic context for assessing assay specificity. Results These synthetic ARMs were experimentally evaluated using several published quantitative PCR (qPCR) assays, including those from the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC), the World Health Organization (WHO), and the World Organization for Animal Health (WOAH). We conducted an in silico assessment of ARM compatibility using over 250 published assays.

The synthetic products performed as well as genomic RNA in all tests. Additionally, the avian influenza ARMs were confirmed to be compatible with subtype-specific loop-mediated isothermal amplification (LAMP) assays and the BioFire Diagnostics FilmArray (bioMérieux) in vitro diagnostic device.
Interpretation and novelty

Our findings indicate that the synthetic RNA ARMs are equivalent to their corresponding genomic RNA, making them a valuable BSL-1 alternative to BSL-3–derived materials.

流感A病毒NS1中相关构象动态的进化网络分析

Network analysis of correlated conformational dynamics across the evolution of influenza A virus NS1
作者： Abhishek Bastiray, James E. Gonzales, Wonmuk Hwang, Jae-Hyun Cho
摘要中文翻译蛋白进化需要序列变化来改变分子特性,同时不破坏其功能所必需的动态组织。如何在残基水平的相关运动网络中容纳这样的突变仍不清楚。在这里,我们研究了1918年至2004年间出现的流感A病毒非结构蛋白1(NS1)变体中相关构象动态的进化。通过分子动力学(MD)模拟,我们构建了残基网络,其中边权重反映了构象运动的广义相关性。使用中心性分析来表征相关动态网络。整体网络特性在NS1变体中保持良好一致性,表明动态网络具有进化稳健性。在多种中心性指标中排名较高的残基形成了蛋白质核心中的主要疏水簇。这些残基表现出较低的构象熵和较高的序列保守性,与刚性、协调的运动一致。相比之下,表现出之间性与接近性中心性排名较大差异的残基占据了围绕核心网络的灵活、溶剂暴露区域。这些残基也显示出较低的进化保守性。这些发现揭示了NS1中一个核心-边缘结构,其中刚性的疏水网络使长程动态连接成为可能,而更具适应性的边缘区域则容纳了进化序列变化。这种架构为长程表型相互作用提供了机制框架,而不会损害其基础结构框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解流感A病毒NS1变体中残基水平的相关运动网络如何容纳突变,同时保持功能动态。背景强调了探索改变分子特性而不破坏功能所必需的动态组织的序列变化的必要性。
设计与对象
本研究调查了1918年至2004年间出现的流感A病毒非结构蛋白1(NS1)变体中相关构象动态的进化。它关注了这些变体在不同时期的结构和功能特性。
核心方法
研究人员使用分子动力学(MD)模拟构建了残基网络,其中边权重反映了构象运动的广义相关性。他们通过中心性分析来表征网络,以确定关键残基及其在动态连接中的作用。
主要结果
相关动态网络在NS1变体中被发现保持良好一致性,表明其具有进化稳健性。在多种中心性指标中排名较高的残基形成了蛋白质核心中的主要疏水簇,表现出较低的构象熵和较高的序列保守性。
解释与创新
这些发现揭示了NS1中的核心-边缘结构,其中刚性的疏水网络使长程动态连接成为可能,而更具适应性的边缘区域则容纳了进化序列变化。这种架构为长程表型相互作用提供了机制框架,而不会损害其基础结构框架。
科研与公卫意义
本研究提供了对流感A病毒NS1结构动态的见解,这可能有助于未来关于病毒进化的研究以及针对季节性流感的定向干预措施的开发。
局限与证据强度
本研究依赖于分子动力学模拟和中心性分析,这可能无法完全捕捉体内动态。证据强度中等,因为它是基于计算模型而非实验验证。

Network analysis of correlated conformational dynamics across the evolution of influenza A virus NS1

Authors: Abhishek Bastiray, James E. Gonzales, Wonmuk Hwang, Jae-Hyun Cho
Original abstract (English)
Protein evolution requires sequence changes to alter molecular properties without disrupting the dynamic organization necessary for function. How residue-level networks of correlated motions accommodate such mutations remains poorly understood. Here, we investigate the evolution of correlated conformational dynamics in nonstructural protein 1 (NS1) variants of influenza A viruses that emerged between 1918 and 2004. From molecular dynamics (MD) simulations we built residue networks in which edge weights reflect generalized correlations of conformational motion. The network of correlated dynamics is characterized using centrality analysis. Overall network properties are well-conserved across NS1 variants, indicating evolutionary robustness of the dynamical network. Residues ranked highly across multiple centrality measures form a predominantly hydrophobic cluster in the protein core. These residues exhibited low conformational entropy and high sequence conservation, consistent with rigid, coordinated motions. In contrast, residues exhibiting large differences in betweenness and closeness centrality ranks occupied flexible, solvent-exposed regions surrounding the core network. These residues also showed lower evolutionary conservation. These findings reveal a core-periphery structure in which a rigid hydrophobic network enables long-range dynamic connectivity while more adaptable peripheral regions accommodate evolutionary sequence variation. This architecture provides a mechanistic framework for long-range epistatic interactions without compromising the underlying structural framework.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how residue-level networks of correlated motions in influenza A virus NS1 variants accommodate mutations while maintaining functional dynamics. The background highlights the need to explore sequence changes that alter molecular properties without disrupting the dynamic organization essential for function.
Design and population
The study investigated the evolution of correlated conformational dynamics in nonstructural protein 1 (NS1) variants of influenza A viruses that emerged between 1918 and 2004. It focused on the structural and functional properties of these variants across different time periods.
Core methods
The researchers used molecular dynamics (MD) simulations to build residue networks, where edge weights reflected generalized correlations of conformational motion. They characterized the network using centrality analysis to identify key residues and their roles in dynamic connectivity.
Main results
The network of correlated dynamics was found to be well-conserved across NS1 variants, indicating evolutionary robustness. Residues ranked highly in multiple centrality measures formed a predominantly hydrophobic cluster in the protein core, showing low conformational entropy and high sequence conservation.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings reveal a core-periphery structure in NS1, where a rigid hydrophobic network enables long-range dynamic connectivity, while more adaptable peripheral regions allow for evolutionary sequence variation. This architecture provides a mechanistic framework for long-range epistatic interactions without compromising the structural framework.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study offers insights into the structural dynamics of influenza A virus NS1, which could inform future research on viral evolution and the development of targeted interventions for seasonal influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on molecular dynamics simulations and centrality analysis, which may not fully capture in vivo dynamics. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on computational models rather than experimental validation.

VirPLM: 基于流感A/H3N2病毒的抗原预测,采用微调的蛋白质语言模型。

VirPLM: antigenic prediction of influenza A/H3N2 viruses with a fine-tuned protein language model.
作者： Xingyi Li, Kexin Xiao, Chunyan Zhou, Xiangting Jia, Dongmin Zhao, Jialuo Xu, Xianying Zeng, Jianzhong Shi, Xuequn Shang, Junnan Zhu
摘要中文翻译MOTIVATION: 人类流感A/H3N2病毒由于血凝素亚单位1(HA1)上的免疫压力而经历快速抗原漂移。在HA1中,氨基酸替换导致抗原漂移,需要频繁更新抗原漂移毒株。尽管血凝素抑制(HI)试验仍然是评估抗原关系的黄金标准,但其劳动密集型和低通量的性质限制了其可扩展性。幸运的是,HA1序列的快速积累使得基于序列的抗原预测成为可能,但有效提取这些病毒序列中的信息性表示仍然是一个挑战。RESULTS: 在本研究中,我们提出了VirPLM,这是一个两阶段框架,通过将ESM-2蛋白质语言模型适应于H3N2 HA1序列来进行抗原预测。VirPLM在抗原预测方面显著优于代表性方法,并且在交叉验证和回顾性时间分割评估中均表现出稳健的性能。此外,VirPLM识别出与抗原漂移高度相关的关键位点。在季节性覆盖分析中,VirPLM优先选择的毒株在大多数评估季节中估计覆盖率高于世界卫生组织推荐的历史毒株,这表明VirPLM可以为候选毒株优先选择提供互补的基于序列的证据。AVAILABILITY AND IMPLEMENTATION: 源代码可在https://github.com/xingyili/VirPLM获取,本研究中使用的版本已存档于Zenodo(DOI: 10.5281/zenodo.21650323)。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在解决流感A/H3N2病毒抗原预测的挑战,这些病毒由于血凝素亚单位1(HA1)上的免疫压力而经历快速抗原漂移。
设计与对象
本研究开发并评估了一个名为VirPLM的两阶段框架,用于基于HA1序列预测流感A/H3N2病毒的抗原特性,其性能通过交叉验证和回顾性时间分割评估进行评估。
核心方法
VirPLM通过将ESM-2蛋白质语言模型适应于H3N2 HA1序列来预测抗原特性,利用HA1序列的快速积累创建信息性表示以进行抗原漂移分析。
主要结果
VirPLM在抗原预测方面显著优于代表性方法,并识别出与抗原漂移高度相关的关键位点。在季节性覆盖分析中,VirPLM优先选择的毒株在大多数评估季节中估计覆盖率高于历史WHO推荐的毒株。
解释与创新
作者将VirPLM解释为一种新颖的工具,能够为候选毒株优先选择提供互补的基于序列的证据,从而提高抗原预测的准确性和可扩展性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,VirPLM可以通过提供更准确和可扩展的抗原预测方法,改进抗原漂移毒株的选择,这对抗原漂移疫苗的开发和公共卫生准备具有直接意义。
局限与证据强度
本研究的证据是强有力的,因为它在多种评估方法中均表现出稳健的性能,但该框架在其他流感亚型或情境下的泛化能力可能受到限制,除非进一步验证。

VirPLM: antigenic prediction of influenza A/H3N2 viruses with a fine-tuned protein language model.

Authors: Xingyi Li, Kexin Xiao, Chunyan Zhou, Xiangting Jia, Dongmin Zhao, Jialuo Xu, Xianying Zeng, Jianzhong Shi, Xuequn Shang, Junnan Zhu
Original abstract (English)
MOTIVATION: Human influenza A/H3N2 viruses undergo rapid antigenic evolution primarily driven by the hemagglutinin subunit 1 (HA1). Within HA1, amino acid substitutions under immune pressure cause antigenic drift, necessitating frequent updates to vaccine strains. While hemagglutination inhibition (HI) assays remain the gold standard for assessing antigenic relationships, their labor-intensive and low-throughput nature limits scalability. Fortunately, the rapid accumulation of HA1 sequences enables sequence-based antigenic prediction, yet effectively extracting informative representations from these viral sequences remains challenging. RESULTS: In this study, we present VirPLM, a two-stage framework that adapts the ESM-2 protein language model to H3N2 HA1 sequences for antigenic prediction. VirPLM significantly outperforms representative methods and maintains robust performance under both cross-validation and retrospective time-split evaluations. Moreover, VirPLM identifies highly critical sites enriched in known regions related to antigenic evolution. In the season-specific coverage analysis, VirPLM-prioritized strains achieve higher estimated coverage rates than the corresponding historical strains recommended by the World Health Organization in most evaluated seasons, suggesting that VirPLM can provide complementary sequence-based evidence for candidate strain prioritization. AVAILABILITY AND IMPLEMENTATION: The source code is available at https://github.com/xingyili/VirPLM, and the version used in this study is archived in Zenodo (DOI: 10.5281/zenodo.21650323).
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenge of antigenic prediction for influenza A/H3N2 viruses, which undergo rapid antigenic evolution due to immune pressure on the hemagglutinin subunit 1 (HA1).
Design and population
The study developed and evaluated a two-stage framework called VirPLM for antigenic prediction of influenza A/H3N2 viruses using HA1 sequences, with performance assessed through cross-validation and retrospective time-split evaluations.
Core methods
VirPLM adapts the ESM-2 protein language model to H3N2 HA1 sequences to predict antigenic properties, leveraging the rapid accumulation of HA1 sequences to create informative representations for antigenic drift analysis.
Main results
VirPLM significantly outperforms representative methods in antigenic prediction and identifies highly critical sites associated with antigenic evolution. In season-specific coverage analysis, strains prioritized by VirPLM achieve higher estimated coverage rates than historical WHO-recommended strains in most evaluated seasons.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret VirPLM as a novel tool that provides complementary sequence-based evidence for candidate strain prioritization, enhancing the accuracy and scalability of antigenic prediction for seasonal influenza.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that VirPLM can improve the selection of vaccine strains by offering a more accurate and scalable method for antigenic prediction, which has direct implications for vaccine development and public health preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is strong, as it demonstrates robust performance across multiple evaluation methods, but the framework's generalizability to other influenza subtypes or contexts may be limited without further validation.

一种主导的基因组广泛氨基酸特征标志了流感甲型H3N2流感病毒克隆K的全球扩张

A Dominant Genome-Wide Amino-Acid Signature Characterizes the Global Expansion of Influenza A(H3N2) Clade K
作者： Ida B. K. Suardana, Heru Susetya, Made Sumitha Kameswari, Sri Masyeni, Juergen A. Richt, Gusti Mahardika
摘要中文翻译摘要 2025年底,流感甲型H3N2流感病毒克隆K病毒在全球监测数据集中的频率增加。为了表征这种出现的分子模式,我们使用地理分层抽样方法,对2025年12月至2026年1月期间收集的病毒进行了基因组广泛分析,并与2019年至2024年的甲型H3N2流感病毒序列进行了数据库范围的历史比较。在多个蛋白质编码开放阅读框(ORF)中,包括聚合酶、表面、基质、核蛋白和非结构蛋白,均鉴定出主要的氨基酸取代。历史数据库范围分析显示,在2019年至2024年期间,这些氨基酸状态的频率存在显著的异质性,从缺失或罕见到已经高度流行。为了检查它们的共同出现,分析的ORF中的多态性氨基酸状态被组合成基因组广泛复合氨基酸特征。在1255株具有完整八段序列数据的病毒中,共鉴定出168种不同的特征。其中一种特征占主导地位,出现在744株(59.3%)病毒中,而第二常见的特征出现在139株(11.1%);其余特征的频率显著较低。这些发现表明,流感甲型H3N2流感病毒克隆K的全球出现特征是通过一种主导的基因组广泛氨基酸特征实现的,该特征由具有显著不同历史频率的氨基酸状态组成。对复合分子特征进行基因组广泛分析为表征快速扩张的流感病毒种群的遗传结构提供了额外的框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在表征与流感甲型H3N2流感病毒克隆K病毒全球出现相关的分子模式,因为这些病毒在2025年底的全球监测数据集中的频率增加。
设计与对象
本研究使用地理分层抽样方法,对2025年12月至2026年1月期间收集的流感甲型H3N2流感病毒病毒进行了基因组广泛分析,并将其与2019年至2024年的甲型H3N2流感病毒序列进行了数据库范围的比较。
核心方法
研究人员在多个蛋白质编码开放阅读框(ORF)中鉴定了主要的氨基酸取代,并将多态性氨基酸状态组合成基因组广泛复合氨基酸特征。
主要结果
在1255株具有完整八段序列数据的病毒中,共鉴定出168种不同的特征,其中一种特征占主导地位,出现在744株(59.3%)病毒中,而第二常见的特征出现在139株(11.1%);其余特征的频率显著较低。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,流感甲型H3N2流感病毒克隆K的全球出现特征是通过一种主导的基因组广泛氨基酸特征实现的,该特征由具有显著不同历史频率的氨基酸状态组成。
科研与公卫意义
本研究为理解快速扩张的流感病毒种群的遗传结构提供了框架,这可以为监测和诊断策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅基于摘要证据,分析基于历史数据库范围的比较,可能无法完全捕捉病毒进化的动态特性。

A Dominant Genome-Wide Amino-Acid Signature Characterizes the Global Expansion of Influenza A(H3N2) Clade K

Authors: Ida B. K. Suardana, Heru Susetya, Made Sumitha Kameswari, Sri Masyeni, Juergen A. Richt, Gusti Mahardika
Original abstract (English)
Abstract In late 2025, influenza A(H3N2) Clade K viruses increased in frequency in global surveillance datasets. To characterize the molecular pattern associated with this emergence, we conducted genome-wide analysis of viruses collected during December 2025–January 2026 using geographically stratified sampling and a database-wide historical comparison with A(H3N2) sequences from 2019–2024. Predominant amino-acid substitutions were identified across multiple protein-coding open reading frames (ORF), including polymerase, surface, matrix, nucleoprotein, and nonstructural proteins. Historical database-wide analysis showed marked heterogeneity in the frequencies of these amino-acid states during 2019–2024, ranging from absent or rare to already highly prevalent. To examine their joint occurrence, polymorphic amino-acid states across the analyzed ORF were combined into genome-wide composite amino-acid signatures. Among 1,255 viruses with complete eight-segment sequence data, 168 distinct signatures were identified. A single signature predominated, occurring in 744 (59.3%) viruses, whereas the second most frequent signature occurred in 139 (11.1%); the remaining signatures were substantially less frequent. These findings demonstrate that the global emergence of influenza A(H3N2) Clade K was characterized by a predominant genome-wide amino-acid signature assembled from amino-acid states with markedly different historical frequencies. Genome-wide analysis of composite molecular signatures provides an additional framework for characterizing the genetic structure of rapidly expanding influenza virus populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize the molecular pattern associated with the global emergence of influenza A(H3N2) Clade K viruses, as they increased in frequency in global surveillance datasets in late 2025.
Design and population
The study conducted genome-wide analysis of influenza A(H3N2) viruses collected during December 2025–January 2026 using geographically stratified sampling and compared them with A(H3N2) sequences from 2019–2024.
Core methods
The researchers identified predominant amino-acid substitutions across multiple protein-coding open reading frames (ORF) and combined polymorphic amino-acid states into genome-wide composite amino-acid signatures.
Main results
Among 1,255 viruses with complete eight-segment sequence data, 168 distinct signatures were identified, with a single signature predominating in 744 (59.3%) viruses, and the second most frequent signature occurring in 139 (11.1%).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings demonstrate that the global emergence of influenza A(H3N2) Clade K was marked by a predominant genome-wide amino-acid signature assembled from amino-acid states with varying historical frequencies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides a framework for understanding the genetic structure of rapidly expanding influenza virus populations, which can inform surveillance and diagnostic strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, and the analysis is based on historical database-wide comparisons, which may not fully capture the dynamic nature of viral evolution.

髓系细胞中维生素D受体(VDR)和甲状旁腺激素(PTH)的表达调控小鼠对流感病毒感染的宿主反应

Myeloid cell expression of the VDR and parathyroid hormone control the host response to influenza infection in mice
作者： Meng-Zhu Tang, Nicole E. Froelich, Nicholas J. Hanna, Veronika C. Weaver, Gustavo A. Nader, Troy C. Sutton, Margherita T. Cantorna
摘要中文翻译引言 由季节性流感病毒引起的呼吸道感染仍然是全球主要的健康负担。维生素D受体(VDR)信号调控免疫应答,但VDR及其与甲状旁腺激素(PTH)的相互作用在宿主抵抗H1N1流感病毒感染中的作用尚不清楚。方法 使用全球VDR敲除(KO)小鼠、髓系(M)VDR敲除(M-VDR KO)小鼠及其相应的野生型(WT)同窝小鼠感染H1N1流感病毒。另外,实验还对WT小鼠进行间歇性PTH或载体处理,随后感染H1N1流感A病毒。评估了疾病严重程度、死亡率、病毒载量和清除、肺部病理以及炎症细胞因子反应。结果 H1N1流感病毒感染WT小鼠导致非致命性疾病,而VDR KO小鼠出现21%的死亡率,病毒清除延迟,症状和肺部病理更严重。呼吸系统疾病严重程度的增加伴随着肺部I型和II型干扰素反应的升高。M-VDR KO小鼠与WT同窝小鼠相比,疾病严重程度增加且病毒清除延迟。接受PTH处理的WT小鼠与接受载体处理的WT小鼠相比,临床评分、流感M基因和细胞因子反应均增加。讨论 本研究描述了一种新的范式,即维生素D-PTH轴通过调控肺部炎症程度和病毒量来控制宿主对流感病毒疾病的抵抗力。此外,数据表明,髓系细胞中VDR的表达对于控制流感病毒的早期先天免疫应答至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解维生素D受体(VDR)和甲状旁腺激素(PTH)在宿主抵抗流感病毒感染中的作用,填补了对流感病毒免疫应答的知识空白。
设计与对象
本研究使用了全球VDR敲除(KO)小鼠、髓系VDR敲除(M-VDR KO)小鼠及其相应的野生型(WT)同窝小鼠,并感染了H1N1流感病毒。另外,实验还涉及对WT小鼠进行间歇性PTH或载体处理,随后感染H1N1流感A病毒。
核心方法
本研究采用全球VDR敲除(KO)小鼠、髓系VDR敲除小鼠及其野生型(WT)同窝小鼠。进行了H1N1流感病毒感染,并对WT小鼠进行了PTH或载体处理的额外实验。
主要结果
H1N1流感病毒感染WT小鼠导致非致命性疾病,而VDR KO小鼠出现21%的死亡率,病毒清除延迟,症状和肺部病理更严重。与WT同窝小鼠相比,M-VDR KO小鼠也表现出更高的疾病严重程度和病毒清除延迟。与接受载体处理的WT小鼠相比,接受PTH处理的WT小鼠临床评分、流感M基因和细胞因子反应均增加。
解释与创新
本研究描述了一种新的范式,即维生素D-PTH轴通过调控肺部炎症程度和病毒量来控制宿主对流感病毒疾病的抵抗力。髓系细胞中VDR的表达对于控制流感病毒的早期先天免疫应答至关重要。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,针对维生素D-PTH轴可能是一种管理流感感染的潜在策略,特别是在髓系细胞中存在VDR缺乏的个体中。这可能对开发新的治疗方案以及理解流感病毒的免疫应答具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于使用了动物模型,这可能无法完全代表人类的反应。样本量较小,且未包括与人类流感感染的直接比较。

Myeloid cell expression of the VDR and parathyroid hormone control the host response to influenza infection in mice

Authors: Meng-Zhu Tang, Nicole E. Froelich, Nicholas J. Hanna, Veronika C. Weaver, Gustavo A. Nader, Troy C. Sutton, Margherita T. Cantorna
Original abstract (English)
Introduction Respiratory infections caused by seasonal influenza viruses remain a major global health burden. Vitamin D receptor (VDR) signaling regulates immune responses, but the role of the VDR and its interaction with parathyroid hormone (PTH) in host resistance to H1N1 influenza infection is not understood. Methods Global VDR knockout (KO) mice, or myeloid (M) deletion of the VDR (M-VDR KO) and the respective wildtype (WT) littermates were infected with H1N1 influenza virus. Additional experiments treated WT mice with intermittent PTH or vehicle and then infected them with a H1N1 influenza A virus. Disease severity, mortality, viral burden and clearance, lung pathology, and inflammatory cytokine responses were evaluated. Results H1N1 influenza infection of WT mice resulted in non-lethal disease, whereas VDR KO mice had 21% mortality, delayed viral clearance, more severe symptoms and lung pathology. The increased severity of respiratory disease was accompanied by higher type I and II interferon responses in the lungs. M-VDR KO mice had increased disease severity and delayed viral clearance compared to WT littermates. PTH-treated WT mice had increased clinical scores, influenza M gene, and cytokine responses compared to vehicle treated WT mice. Discussion The work describes a novel paradigm in which the vitamin D-PTH axis governs host resistance to influenza virus disease by controlling the amount of inflammation and the amount of virus in the lung. In addition, the data suggest that VDR expression in myeloid cells is critical for controlling the early innate immune response to influenza viruses.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand the role of the vitamin D receptor (VDR) and parathyroid hormone (PTH) in host resistance to influenza infection, addressing a knowledge gap in the immunological response to influenza viruses.
Design and population
The study used global VDR knockout (KO) mice, myeloid VDR deletion (M-VDR KO) mice, and their wildtype (WT) littermates, infected with H1N1 influenza virus. Additional experiments involved treating WT mice with intermittent PTH or vehicle and then infecting them with a H1N1 influenza A virus.
Core methods
The study employed global VDR KO mice, myeloid VDR deletion mice, and WT littermates. Infection with H1N1 influenza virus was performed, and additional experiments involved treating WT mice with PTH or vehicle.
Main results
H1N1 influenza infection in WT mice resulted in non-lethal disease, whereas VDR KO mice had 21% mortality, delayed viral clearance, more severe symptoms and lung pathology. M-VDR KO mice also had increased disease severity and delayed viral clearance compared to WT littermates. PTH-treated WT mice had increased clinical scores, influenza M gene, and cytokine responses compared to vehicle-treated WT mice.
Interpretation and novelty
The work describes a novel paradigm in which the vitamin D-PTH axis governs host resistance to influenza virus disease by controlling the amount of inflammation and the amount of virus in the lung. VDR expression in myeloid cells is critical for controlling the early innate immune response to influenza viruses.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeting the vitamin D-PTH axis could be a potential strategy for managing influenza infection, particularly in individuals with VDR deficiency in myeloid cells. This could have implications for the development of new therapeutic approaches and understanding the immune response to influenza.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by the use of animal models, which may not fully represent human responses. The sample size was small, and the study did not include direct comparisons with human influenza infection.

利用历史数据和时间序列分析预测季节性流感模式和趋势

Forecasting Seasonal Influenza Patterns and Trends Using Historical Data and Time Series Analysis
作者： Nimisha Rawat, Zeynep Ertem
摘要中文翻译摘要 有效预测季节性流感模式对于公共卫生准备至关重要,因为流感仍然是一个反复出现的威胁,具有显著的发病率和死亡率,尤其是在易感人群中。尽管持续进行监测工作,该病毒的高突变率以及新毒株的出现使得准确预测暴发的时间和严重程度变得复杂。早期检测和强大的流感监测对于使医疗系统能够做出响应以及缓解季节性资源压力至关重要。本研究利用历史数据和时间序列分析来识别流感传播模式并预测暴发峰值。关键发现表明,结合多元和集成预测模型——如自回归积分滑动平均(ARIMA)、贝叶斯推断和机器学习方法——显著提高了预测的准确性。值得注意的是,将流行病学数据与搜索引擎趋势和环境变量相结合,可以构建一个更全面且实时的预测模型,从而实现更准确和及时的预测。这些方法还能捕捉地区差异并适应新兴趋势,提高暴发准备的稳健性。本研究的意义在于其潜在能力,可以增强公共卫生响应,实现早期检测和资源分配,改善疫苗接种时间安排,最终减少与流感相关的发病率和死亡率。由于流感仍然是一个持续的全球性威胁,本研究的方法强调了在流行病学预测中整合先进预测工具和实时数据的重要性,为更有效的健康干预策略奠定了基础。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在改进季节性流感模式的预测,以增强公共卫生准备,因为流感仍然是一个反复出现的威胁,具有显著的发病率和死亡率,尤其是在易感人群中。
设计与对象
本研究利用历史数据和时间序列分析来识别流感传播模式并预测暴发峰值,重点关注季节性流感病毒及其对公共卫生系统的影响。
核心方法
本研究结合了多元和集成预测模型,包括自回归积分滑动平均(ARIMA)、贝叶斯推断和机器学习方法,以分析流感数据。
主要结果
关键发现表明,将流行病学数据与搜索引擎趋势和环境变量相结合,显著提高了流感暴发预测的准确性和及时性。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为一种新颖的流感预测方法,证明结合多种数据来源可以增强模型对暴发时间和严重程度的预测能力。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以通过实现早期检测、更好地分配资源以及改善疫苗接种活动的时间安排,为更有效的监测和干预策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
本研究的证据仅基于摘要,限制了分析的深度,但所描述的方法是稳健的,且结果得到了数据来源的全面整合支持,这表明对研究结果有中等程度的信心。

Forecasting Seasonal Influenza Patterns and Trends Using Historical Data and Time Series Analysis

Authors: Nimisha Rawat, Zeynep Ertem
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Effective forecasting of seasonal influenza patterns is crucial for public health preparedness, as influenza remains a recurrent threat with considerable morbidity and mortality, especially among vulnerable populations. The virus's high mutation rate and the emergence of new strains complicate accurate prediction of outbreak timing and severity, despite ongoing surveillance efforts. Early detection and robust influenza surveillance are essential for equipping healthcare systems to respond and for alleviating the seasonal strain on resources. This study leverages historical data and time series analysis to identify patterns in influenza spread and predict outbreak peaks. Key findings reveal that incorporating multivariate and ensemble forecasting models—such as autoregressive integrated moving average (ARIMA), Bayesian inference, and machine learning approaches—substantially improves prediction accuracy. Notably, combining epidemiological data with search engine trends and environmental variables yields a more comprehensive and real-time forecast model, enabling more accurate and timely predictions. These methods also capture regional variations and can adapt to emerging trends, increasing the robustness of outbreak preparedness. The significance of this research lies in its potential to enhance public health response by enabling early detection and resource allocation, improving vaccination timing, and ultimately reducing influenza-related morbidity and mortality. With influenza remaining a persistent global threat, this study’s approach underscores the importance of integrating advanced predictive tools and real-time data in epidemiological forecasting, providing a foundation for more effective health intervention strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to improve the forecasting of seasonal influenza patterns to enhance public health preparedness, as influenza remains a recurrent threat with significant morbidity and mortality, particularly among vulnerable populations.
Design and population
This study utilized historical data and time series analysis to identify patterns in influenza spread and predict outbreak peaks, focusing on seasonal influenza viruses and their impact on public health systems.
Core methods
The study incorporated multivariate and ensemble forecasting models, including autoregressive integrated moving average (ARIMA), Bayesian inference, and machine learning approaches, to analyze influenza data.
Main results
Key findings revealed that integrating epidemiological data with search engine trends and environmental variables significantly improved the accuracy and timeliness of influenza outbreak predictions.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted these findings as a novel approach to influenza forecasting, demonstrating that combining diverse data sources enhances the predictive power of models for outbreak timing and severity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform more effective surveillance and intervention strategies by enabling early detection, better resource allocation, and improved timing for vaccination campaigns.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's abstract-only evidence limits the depth of analysis, but the methods described are robust and the results are supported by a comprehensive integration of data sources, suggesting moderate confidence in the findings.

季节性流感率与医院获得性艰难梭菌感染率的相关性:对研究和预防干预的启示

Correlation of seasonal influenza rates with hospital-acquired Clostridioides difficile rates: Implications for research and interventions for prevention
作者： Peter Faris, Jenine Leal, Nicole Lamont, Wrechelle Ocampo, Jayna Holroyd-Leduc, Deana Sabuda, Bruce Dalton, Jaime Kaufman, Bayan Missaghi, Joseph Kim
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估医院获得性艰难梭菌感染(HA-CDI)的季节性趋势是否与流感率相关,以应对HA-CDI率波动评估中的挑战。
设计与对象
本研究考察了2016年1月至2018年12月期间四家急性护理机构中每10,000个患者日的HA-CDI率和每1,000次入院的流感住院率。
核心方法
在R(4.5.1)中使用了四种统计方法,包括自回归积分滑动平均(ARIMA)、交叉相关分析、泊松谐波回归模型以及包含流感率的泊松谐波回归模型。
主要结果
Cross-correlation analysis found evidence of correlation between HA-CDI and influenza rates at a lag of zero (R = 0.41, 95% CI 0.08 to 0.65), suggesting a seasonal relationship. Poisson harmonic regression models indicated that influenza rates predicted CDI rates.
解释与创新
本研究提供了一种识别HA-CDI率季节性变化的敏感方法,其季节性效应可能由流感入院率所解释,这可能通过不适当的抗生素处方而介导。
科研与公卫意义
这一观察结果可能有助于未来减少HA-CDI率的干预措施的解读,强调在HA-CDI预防策略中考虑流感趋势的重要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究的设计局限性,包括使用统计方法和研究的观察性质,可能会影响研究结果的普遍适用性。

Correlation of seasonal influenza rates with hospital-acquired Clostridioides difficile rates: Implications for research and interventions for prevention

Authors: Peter Faris, Jenine Leal, Nicole Lamont, Wrechelle Ocampo, Jayna Holroyd-Leduc, Deana Sabuda, Bruce Dalton, Jaime Kaufman, Bayan Missaghi, Joseph Kim
Original abstract (English)
Introduction: Hospital-acquired Clostridioides difficile infection (HA-CDI) rate variability can create challenges for assessing interventions that might improve rates. We therefore sought to assess whether seasonal trends in HA-CDI could be related to influenza rates by examining the sensitivity and applicability of statistical methods that identify seasonal trend associations. Methods: We assessed HA-CDI rates per 10,000 patient-days and influenza hospitalizations per 1,000 admissions in four acute care facilities from January 2016 to December 2018. Four statistical approaches were used in R (4.5.1) including (1) autoregressive integrated moving average (ARIMA); (2) cross-correlation to assess if dependencies lagged over time; (3) Poisson harmonic regression to model cyclical trends; and (4) Poisson harmonic regression models including influenza rates. Results: ARIMA approaches did not detect seasonality in HA-CDI rates, but found strong seasonality in the influenza rates, whereas cross-correlation analysis found evidence of correlation between the series at a lag of zero (R = 0.41, 95% CI 0.08 to 0.65) and provided an indication of a seasonal relationship. Poisson harmonic regression for trends suggested that influenza rates predicted CDI rates ( p p p Conclusions: Poisson harmonic regression provided a sensitive means of identifying seasonality in HA-CDI rates with the seasonality effect being accounted for by influenza admission rates, which may be mediated by inappropriate antibiotic prescriptions. This observation may be useful in interpreting future interventions to reduce HA-CDI.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to assess whether seasonal trends in hospital-acquired Clostridioides difficile infection (HA-CDI) could be related to influenza rates, addressing challenges in assessing interventions for HA-CDI rates variability.
Design and population
The study examined HA-CDI rates per 10,000 patient-days and influenza hospitalizations per 1,000 admissions in four acute care facilities from January 2016 to December 2018.
Core methods
Four statistical approaches were used in R (4.5.1) including autoregressive integrated moving average (ARIMA), cross-correlation, Poisson harmonic regression, and Poisson harmonic regression models including influenza rates.
Main results
Cross-correlation analysis found evidence of correlation between HA-CDI and influenza rates at a lag of zero (R = 0.41, 95% CI 0.08 to 0.65), suggesting a seasonal relationship. Poisson harmonic regression models indicated that influenza rates predicted CDI rates.
Interpretation and novelty
The study provided a sensitive means of identifying seasonality in HA-CDI rates, with seasonality effects accounted for by influenza admission rates, potentially mediated by inappropriate antibiotic prescriptions.
Scientific and public-health significance
This observation may be useful in interpreting future interventions to reduce HA-CDI rates, highlighting the importance of considering influenza trends in HA-CDI prevention strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's design limitations, including the use of statistical methods and the observational nature of the study, may affect the generalizability of the findings.

N6-甲基腺苷(m6A)RNA修饰通过限制IFITM2/3表达调控抗病毒药物对流感A病毒感染的反应

N6-methyladenosine (m6A) RNA modifications control the antiviral response to influenza A virus infection by limiting IFITM2/3 expression
作者： Piotr Kwiatkowski, Liudmila A Mikheeva, Holly Digby, Clément R. Bouton, Hannah Jones, Stuart J. D. Neil, Michelle L. Holland, Chad M. Swanson
摘要中文翻译N6-甲基腺苷(m6A)RNA修饰调控多种细胞过程,包括对病毒感染的反应。然而,m6A如何调控抗病毒药物基因表达,以及其对病毒复制的影响是否源于病毒RNA的直接修饰或对细胞转录本的调控仍不清楚。在此研究中,我们分析了m6A修饰如何调控细胞自主的先天免疫应答对流感A病毒(IAV)感染的反应。METTL3或YTHDF2的耗尽或抑制会限制IAV复制。然而,使用定量方法识别m6A位点后,我们并未检测到病毒RNA的显著m6A修饰。相反,许多由IAV诱导的细胞转录本在其编码区被m6A修饰,包括编码模式识别受体和直接抗病毒药物蛋白的转录本。干扰素信号通路的抑制可恢复METTL3抑制后的IAV复制,这表明m6A调控了抗病毒药物对感染的反应。METTL3的抑制增加了同源抗病毒药物蛋白IFITM2和IFITM3的表达,这些蛋白对于METTL3抑制剂的抗病毒药物活性是必需的。我们提出,m6A修饰通过调控抗病毒药物蛋白的表达来限制其诱导的幅度,并在维持细胞稳态的同时平衡抗病毒药物活性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解N6-甲基腺苷(m6A)RNA修饰如何调控抗病毒药物对流感A病毒(IAV)感染的反应,填补了关于m6A调控抗病毒药物基因表达机制及其对病毒复制影响的知识空白。
设计与对象
本研究分析了m6A修饰在IAV感染的先天免疫应答中的作用,重点关注细胞转录本而非病毒RNA。
核心方法
研究人员使用定量方法识别m6A位点,并抑制m6A写入因子METTL3和m6A读取因子YTHDF2,以评估其对IAV复制和抗病毒药物蛋白表达的影响。
主要结果
METTL3或YTHDF2的耗尽或抑制限制了IAV复制,而METTL3的抑制增加了同源抗病毒药物蛋白IFITM2和IFITM3的表达,这些蛋白对于METTL3抑制剂的抗病毒药物活性是必需的。
解释与创新
本研究提出,m6A修饰通过调控抗病毒药物蛋白的表达来限制其诱导的幅度,并在维持细胞稳态的同时平衡抗病毒药物活性,突显了m6A在调控抗病毒药物对IAV感染反应中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,针对m6A修饰可能是调控抗病毒药物反应的一种潜在策略,并可能对抗病毒药物治疗的发展具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性包括仅关注细胞转录本而非病毒RNA,以及使用定量方法识别m6A位点,这可能无法完全反映IAV感染中m6A调控的复杂性。}}.{

N6-methyladenosine (m6A) RNA modifications control the antiviral response to influenza A virus infection by limiting IFITM2/3 expression

Authors: Piotr Kwiatkowski, Liudmila A Mikheeva, Holly Digby, Clément R. Bouton, Hannah Jones, Stuart J. D. Neil, Michelle L. Holland, Chad M. Swanson
Original abstract (English)
N6-methyladenosine (m6A) RNA modifications regulate diverse cellular processes, including the response to virus infection. However, how m6A controls antiviral gene expression and whether effects on viral replication are due to direct modification of viral RNA or regulation of cellular transcripts remain unclear. Herein, we analysed how m6A modifications control the cell-autonomous innate immune response to influenza A virus (IAV) infection. Depletion or inhibition of the m6A writer METTL3 or the m6A reader YTHDF2 restricts IAV replication. However, using a quantitative method to identify m6A sites, we did not detect substantial m6A modification of viral RNA. In contrast, many IAV-induced cellular transcripts were m6A modified in their coding region, including those that encode pattern recognition receptors and direct antiviral proteins. Inhibition of interferon signalling restored IAV replication after METTL3 inhibition, highlighting that m6A controls the antiviral response to infection. METTL3 inhibition increased expression of the paralogous antiviral proteins IFITM2 and IFITM3, which were required for the antiviral activity of the METTL3 inhibitor. We propose that m6A modifications regulate the expression of antiviral proteins to limit the magnitude of their induction and balance antiviral activity with maintaining cellular homeostasis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand how N6-methyladenosine (m6A) RNA modifications control the antiviral response to influenza A virus (IAV) infection, addressing gaps in knowledge about the mechanisms of m6A regulation of antiviral gene expression and the impact on viral replication.
Design and population
The study analyzed the role of m6A modifications in the innate immune response to IAV infection, focusing on cellular transcripts rather than viral RNA.
Core methods
The researchers used quantitative methods to identify m6A sites and inhibited the m6A writer METTL3 and the m6A reader YTHDF2 to assess their effects on IAV replication and antiviral protein expression.
Main results
Depletion or inhibition of METTL3 or YTHDF2 restricted IAV replication, and METTL3 inhibition increased the expression of antiviral proteins IFITM2 and IFITM3, which were required for the antiviral activity of the METTL3 inhibitor.
Interpretation and novelty
The study proposes that m6A modifications regulate the expression of antiviral proteins to limit their induction and balance antiviral activity with maintaining cellular homeostasis, highlighting the importance of m6A in controlling the antiviral response to IAV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeting m6A modifications could be a potential strategy for modulating the antiviral response and could have implications for the development of antiviral therapies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's limitations include the focus on cellular transcripts rather than viral RNA and the use of quantitative methods to identify m6A sites, which may not fully capture the complexity of m6A regulation in IAV infection.

B-312 流感A病毒通用基因型鉴定:一种使用通用片段引物的敏捷牛津纳米孔测序工作流程

B-312 Universal genotyping of Influenza A virus: an agile Oxford Nanopore Sequencing workflow using universal segment primers
作者： Gustavo Barra, Lucas Pereira, Aline Belmok, Marco Oliveira, Fabián Erazo, Miguel Andrade, Hillary Magalhães, Vinicius Monteiro, Lara Franciele Ribeiro Velasco, Rafael Henriques Jácomo
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 流感A病毒是一种具有八个基因组片段的分节负链RNA病毒。对分子特征进行高效鉴定对于公共卫生决策至关重要。针对保守末端区域的通用引物能够在单一工作流程中扩增所有片段,支持快速测序和基因型分配。本研究旨在标准化并评估一种针对流感A病毒基因型鉴定的通用引物牛津纳米孔测序工作流程,包括实时监测和运行后分支分配。方法 使用2025年10月至11月期间通过qPCR检测出流感A病毒的呼吸道样本残余洗脱液进行基因组监测。样本去标识化后,使用High-Capacity cDNA Reverse Transcription Kit进行cDNA合成,并补充针对保守末端区域的通用引物。使用相同的引物集合和Q5® High-Fidelity 2X Master Mix进行扩增子富集;产物通过琼脂糖凝胶电泳进行评估。条形码使用基于转座的策略,库通过接头连接,测序在MinION流细胞上进行。实时监测使用RAMPART进行。共识生成在EPI2ME中完成,随后使用Nextclade对流感甲型H3N2流感病毒的HA和NA进行分支/基因型分析,参考A/Darwin/6/2021。结果 对95个样本进行了5小时31分钟的测序,生成了752,910条读数。根据预定义的标准(最小20×深度和=75%水平基因组覆盖),71/95个样本符合已建立的指标。4个样本未产生任何映射读数。其余20个样本产生了部分基因组恢复,平均水平覆盖率为52%(范围8.08%–74.94%)。尽管部分样本未能实现完整的基因组恢复,仍有87/95个样本(91.6%)实现了分支水平的基因型鉴定,表明大多数样本获得了足够的亚型/分支信息序列。8个样本未进行基因型鉴定:其中4个因无映射读数,另外4个因覆盖不足而无法进行可靠分配。结论 这种通用引物的纳米孔测序工作流程能够在单次MinION运行中实现快速、高通量的流感A病毒基因型鉴定,同时具备实时监测和高分支分配成功率(91.6%)。20×深度和=75%水平覆盖的质控阈值有效识别了适合近完全基因组恢复的样本,尽管输入质量存在异质性,大多数样本仍能进行分支分配。这种方法支持临床实验室的敏捷监测,并具有可扩展的实施能力。

问题与背景
本研究旨在标准化并评估一种针对流感A病毒基因型鉴定的通用引物牛津纳米孔测序工作流程,包括实时监测和运行后分支分配。
设计与对象
样本去标识化后,使用High-Capacity cDNA Reverse Transcription Kit进行cDNA合成,并补充针对保守末端区域的通用引物。使用2025年10月至11月期间通过qPCR检测出流感A病毒的呼吸道样本残余洗脱液进行基因组监测。
核心方法
针对保守末端区域的通用引物能够在单一工作流程中扩增所有片段,支持快速测序和基因型分配。使用相同的引物集合和Q5® High-Fidelity 2X Master Mix进行扩增子富集;产物通过琼脂糖凝胶电泳进行评估。条形码使用基于转座的策略,库通过接头连接,测序在MinION流细胞上进行。
主要结果
根据预定义的标准(最小20×深度和=75%水平基因组覆盖),71/95个样本符合已建立的指标。4个样本未产生任何映射读数。其余20个样本产生了部分基因组恢复,平均水平覆盖率为52%(范围8.08%–74.94%)。尽管部分样本未能实现完整的基因组恢复,仍有87/95个样本(91.6%)实现了分支水平的基因型鉴定,表明大多数样本获得了足够的亚型/分支信息序列。
解释与创新
这种通用引物的纳米孔测序工作流程能够在单次MinION运行中实现快速、高通量的流感A病毒基因型鉴定,同时具备实时监测和高分支分配成功率(91.6%)。
科研与公卫意义
这种方法支持临床实验室的敏捷监测,并具有可扩展的实施能力,能够实现对分子监测和公共卫生决策的高效基因组鉴定。
局限与证据强度
20×深度和=75%水平覆盖的质控阈值有效识别了适合近完全基因组恢复的样本,尽管输入质量存在异质性,大多数样本仍能进行分支分配。研究中未报告具体的工作流程设计局限性。

B-312 Universal genotyping of Influenza A virus: an agile Oxford Nanopore Sequencing workflow using universal segment primers

Authors: Gustavo Barra, Lucas Pereira, Aline Belmok, Marco Oliveira, Fabián Erazo, Miguel Andrade, Hillary Magalhães, Vinicius Monteiro, Lara Franciele Ribeiro Velasco, Rafael Henriques Jácomo
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Influenza A is a segmented negative-sense RNA virus with eight genomic segments. Efficient genome characterization is essential for molecular surveillance and public health decision-making. Universal primers targeting conserved terminal regions enable amplification of all segments within a single workflow, supporting rapid sequencing and genotype assignment. This study aimed to standardize and evaluate a universal-primer Oxford Nanopore workflow for Influenza A genotyping, including real-time monitoring and post-run clade assignment. Methods Residual eluates from respiratory samples collected between October and November 2025 that tested positive for Influenza A by qPCR were used for genomic surveillance. Samples were de-identified and submitted to cDNA synthesis (High-Capacity cDNA Reverse Transcription Kit) supplemented with universal primers targeting conserved terminal regions. Amplicon enrichment was performed with the same primer sets and Q5® High-Fidelity 2X Master Mix; products were assessed by agarose gel electrophoresis. Barcoding used a transposition-based approach, libraries were adapter-ligated, and sequencing was performed on MinION flowcells. Real-time monitoring was performed with RAMPART. Consensus generation was performed in EPI2ME, followed by clade/genotype analysis using Nextclade for Influenza A(H3N2) HA and NA against reference A/Darwin/6/2021. Results A library of 95 samples was sequenced for 5 hours and 31 minutes, generating 752,910 reads. Using predefined criteria for near-complete genome recovery (minimum 20× depth and =75% horizontal genome coverage), 71/95 samples met the established metrics. Four samples produced no mapped reads. The remaining 20 samples yielded partial genome recovery with mean horizontal coverage of 52% (range 8.08%–74.94%). Despite incomplete whole-genome recovery in a subset, clade-level genotyping was achieved for 87/95 samples (91.6%), indicating that sufficient subtype/clade-informative sequence was obtained for most specimens. Eight samples were not genotyped: four due to absence of mapped reads and four due to insufficient coverage to support confident assignment. Conclusion This universal-primer Nanopore workflow enabled rapid, high-throughput Influenza A genotyping with real-time monitoring and high clade-assignment success (91.6%) in a single MinION run. QC thresholds of 20× depth and =75% horizontal coverage effectively identified samples suitable for near-complete genome recovery, while clade assignment remained feasible for most samples despite heterogeneous input quality. This approach supports agile clinical-laboratory surveillance with scalable implementation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to standardize and evaluate a universal-primer Oxford Nanopore workflow for Influenza A genotyping, including real-time monitoring and post-run clade assignment.

The supplied evidence does not report specific limitations in the study design.
