B 常规重要学术文献 · 研究B routine priorityAcademic literature · Research

关于冠状病毒病2019年(COVID-19)和季节性流感疫苗和疫苗接种在一般人群和医疗专业人员中的态度和做法

ATTITUDES AND PRACTICES TOWARDS COVID-19 AND SEASONAL FLU VACCINES AND VACCINATION IN THE GENERAL POPULATION AND AMONG MEDICAL PROFESSIONALS
作者： Katarina Petković, D. Krajnović, Andijana Milošević-Georgijev, B. Filipić, Andjela Marinković, Valentina Marinković
摘要中文翻译冠状病毒病2019年(COVID-19)大流行促使开发了多种类型的疫苗,其中基于信使核糖核酸(mRNA)的疫苗代表了重要的突破。本研究旨在评估一般人群和具有医疗背景的个体对冠状病毒病2019年(COVID-19)和季节性流感 疫苗接种的态度。设计了一份问卷,用于收集参与者的社会人口学数据、个人健康信息、疫苗接种状态以及他们对疫苗的态度和知识。典型参与者是一名在城市地区与他人同住的受过高等教育的女性雇员,报告其总体健康状况良好。典型参与者已接种至少两剂的卫星V(Sputnik V)疫苗。参与者普遍对定期接种冠状病毒病2019年(COVID-19)疫苗接种表现出积极态度。结果显示,具有和不具有医疗背景的参与者在疫苗选择、是否决定接种第三剂或季节性流感 疫苗接种方面没有明显差异。同样,具有和不具有医疗背景的受访者在对阿斯利康(AstraZeneca)疫苗或强制性冠状病毒病2019年(COVID-19)疫苗接种的态度方面也没有明显差异。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估普通人群和有医学背景的人群对 COVID-19 和 季节性流感 疫苗接种 的态度。
设计与对象
参与者普遍对定期进行 COVID-19 疫苗接种 表达出积极态度。典型参与者的特征是一名有 tertiary 教育背景的在职女性,居住在城市地区,与另一个人同住,并报告总体健康状况良好。
核心方法
开发了一份问卷,用于收集社会人口学数据、个人健康信息、疫苗接种状态以及参与者对疫苗的态度和知识情况。
主要结果
结果显示,具有和不具有医疗背景的参与者在疫苗选择、是否决定接种第三剂或季节性流感 疫苗接种方面没有明显差异。
解释与创新
作者将研究结果解释为表明,对疫苗接种的态度在医疗专业人员和普通人群中相似,突出了关于公众对疫苗看法的一个新观察。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为公共卫生策略提供信息,表明在促进疫苗接种时,有针对性的沟通努力可能不需要在医疗专业人员和普通人群之间进行区分。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其依赖于自我报告数据以及仅限摘要的性质,这可能会影响证据的强度。

ATTITUDES AND PRACTICES TOWARDS COVID-19 AND SEASONAL FLU VACCINES AND VACCINATION IN THE GENERAL POPULATION AND AMONG MEDICAL PROFESSIONALS

Authors: Katarina Petković, D. Krajnović, Andijana Milošević-Georgijev, B. Filipić, Andjela Marinković, Valentina Marinković
Original abstract (English)
The COVID-19 pandemic has led to the development of several types of vaccines, among which messenger RNA (mRNA)-based vaccines have represented a significant breakthrough. This study aimed to evaluate attitudes towards COVID-19 and seasonal influenza vaccination among the general population and individuals with a medical background. A questionnaire was developed to collect sociodemographic data, personal health information, vaccination status, and information on participants’ attitudes towards and knowledge of vaccines. The profile of the typical participant was an employed woman with tertiary education, living with another person in an urban area and reporting generally good health. The typical participant had received at least two doses of the Sputnik V vaccine. Participants generally expressed favourable attitudes towards regular COVID-19 vaccination. The results showed no apparent differences between participants with and without a medical background regarding vaccine choice, the decision to receive a third dose, or seasonal influenza vaccination. Similarly, no apparent differences were observed in attitudes towards the AstraZeneca vaccine or mandatory COVID-19 vaccination between respondents with and without a medical background.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate attitudes towards COVID-19 and seasonal influenza vaccination among the general population and individuals with a medical background.
Design and population
Participants generally expressed favourable attitudes towards regular COVID-19 vaccination. The profile of the typical participant was an employed woman with tertiary education, living with another person in an urban area and reporting generally good health.
Core methods
A questionnaire was developed to collect sociodemographic data, personal health information, vaccination status, and information on participants’ attitudes towards and knowledge of vaccines.
Main results
The results showed no apparent differences between participants with and without a medical background regarding vaccine choice, the decision to receive a third dose, or seasonal influenza vaccination.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as indicating that attitudes towards vaccination are similar across medical professionals and the general population, highlighting a novel observation about public perception of vaccines.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform public health strategies by suggesting that targeted communication efforts may not need to differentiate between medical professionals and the general population in promoting vaccination.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its reliance on self-reported data and its abstract-only nature, which may affect the strength of the evidence.

飞机废水病毒组监测揭示旅行者中的病毒多样性:一项来自印度古吉拉特邦的可行性研究。

Aircraft wastewater virome surveillance from domestic and international flights reveals viral diversity among travellers: A proof-of-concept study from Gujarat, India.
作者： Nitin Shukla, Nimesh Patel, Jinal M. Thakor, Harshal Purohit, Priyanka Chavda, Harshil Patel, Madhvi N. Joshi, Chaitanya G. Joshi, Snehal Bagatharia, Varun Shah
摘要中文翻译背景:机场的飞机废水监测(AWS)是一种新兴的检测国际和国内旅行者传播病原体的方法。通过航空旅行,传染病在全球范围内迅速传播,而在机场实施监测系统有助于识别新出现的病毒并大规模追踪病原体。我们旨在表征在古吉拉特邦艾哈迈达巴德机场传播的旅行者病毒多样性,这可能代表常规病毒循环或活跃感染。方法:我们从国内和国际航站楼采集废水样本,其中国内航班连接至18个州和3个联邦直辖区,而国际航班则运营至11个国家的16个目的地。样本采集时间为2024年6月至2025年11月。我们采用基于捕获探针的富集方法对病毒组监测进行分析,并检测了多种具有公共卫生重要性的病毒。结果:我们共鉴定出40个病毒家族和315种病毒,国内废水样本中每份检测到的基因组/片段中位数为71个,国际废水样本中为48.5个。我们检测到了多种呼吸道和肠道病毒,包括甲型流感病毒、SARS-CoV-2、人腺病毒A型、人腺病毒B型、诺如病毒、A型轮状病毒、甲型肝炎病毒、MLB1型星状病毒、人腺病毒F型。其中,某些病毒在国内外废水样本中表现出相对较高的平均RPKMF丰度,例如国际废水样本中检测到的牛轮状病毒A型、人轮状病毒ID45/2型和BK多瘤病毒,而A型甲型肝炎病毒、诺如病毒GII.2型和人类免疫缺陷病毒1型则在国内外废水样本中丰度较高。我们还检测到了多种流感病毒亚型,包括甲型流感病毒H1N1、H3N2、H5N1、H7N1和H9N2亚型,以及乙型和丙型流感病毒。结论:持续的监测可以为公共卫生部门提供早期预警信号,及时的决策和协调响应可以减少疾病的传播,这也需要与国家和国际卫生部门合作。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
飞机废水监测(AWS)在机场是一种新兴的检测国际和国内旅行者传播病原体的方法。通过航空旅行,传染病在全球范围内迅速传播,而在机场实施监测系统有助于识别新出现的病毒并大规模追踪病原体。
设计与对象
我们旨在表征在古吉拉特邦艾哈迈达巴德机场传播的旅行者病毒多样性,这可能代表常规病毒循环或活跃感染。我们从国内和国际航站楼采集废水样本,其中国内航班连接至18个州和3个联邦直辖区,而国际航班则运营至11个国家的16个目的地。
核心方法
我们采用基于捕获探针的富集方法对病毒组监测进行分析,并检测了多种具有公共卫生重要性的病毒。样本采集时间为2024年6月至2025年11月。
主要结果
我们共鉴定出40个病毒家族和315种病毒,国内废水样本中每份检测到的基因组/片段中位数为71个,国际废水样本中为48.5个。我们检测到了多种呼吸道和肠道病毒,包括甲型流感病毒、SARS-CoV-2、人腺病毒A型、人腺病毒B型、诺如病毒、A型轮状病毒、甲型肝炎病毒、MLB1型星状病毒、人腺病毒F型。

科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,机场的废水监测可以成为监测病毒多样性和检测新出现病原体的宝贵工具,这可能有助于制定疾病预防和控制的公共卫生策略。

所提供的证据未报告该研究的局限性或证据强度。

Aircraft wastewater virome surveillance from domestic and international flights reveals viral diversity among travellers: A proof-of-concept study from Gujarat, India.

Authors: Nitin Shukla, Nimesh Patel, Jinal M. Thakor, Harshal Purohit, Priyanka Chavda, Harshil Patel, Madhvi N. Joshi, Chaitanya G. Joshi, Snehal Bagatharia, Varun Shah
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Aircraft wastewater surveillance (AWS) at airports is an emerging method for detecting pathogens shed by international and domestic travellers. Global spread of infectious diseases occurs rapidly through air travel, and implementing surveillance systems at airports can help identify emerging viruses and track pathogens at scale. We aimed to characterize the viral diversity shed by travellers, which may represent routine viral circulation or active infection, at Ahmedabad airport in Gujarat. METHODS: We collected wastewater samples from domestic and international terminals where domestic flights connect to 18 states and 3 Union Territories, while international flights operate across 16 destinations in 11 countries. Samples were collected from June 2024 to November 2025. We used a capture probe-based enrichment approach for virome surveillance and detected multiple viruses of public health importance. RESULTS: We identified a total of 40 viral families and 315 viral species, with a median of 71 genomes/segments detected per sample in domestic wastewater and 48.5 genomes/segments per sample in international wastewater. We detected several respiratory and enteric viruses, including Influenza A virus, SARS-CoV-2, Human mastadenovirus A, Human mastadenovirus B, Noroviruses, Rotavirus A, Hepatitis A virus, Astrovirus MLB1, Human mastadenovirus F. Among these, certain viruses showed relatively higher mean RPKMF abundance in either domestic or international wastewater samples such as Bovine rotavirus A, Human rotavirus ID45/2, and BK polyomavirus observed in international wastewater samples, whereas Aichivirus A1, Norovirus GII.2, and Human immunodeficiency virus 1 showed higher abundance in domestic wastewater samples. We also detected multiple Influenza virus subtypes, including Influenza A subtypes H1N1, H3N2, H5N1, H7N1, and H9N2, along with Influenza B and Influenza C viruses. CONCLUSIONS: Continuous surveillance can provide early warning signals to public health authorities, timely decision-making and coordinated responses can reduce the spread of disease which also requires collaboration with national and international health authorities.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Aircraft wastewater surveillance (AWS) at airports is an emerging method for detecting pathogens shed by international and domestic travellers. Global spread of infectious diseases occurs rapidly through air travel, and implementing surveillance systems at airports can help identify emerging viruses and track pathogens at scale.

We identified a total of 40 viral families and 315 viral species, with a median of 71 genomes/segments detected per sample in domestic wastewater and 48.5 genomes/segments per sample in international wastewater. We detected several respiratory and enteric viruses, including Influenza A virus, SARS-CoV-2, Human mastadenovirus A, Human mastadenovirus B, Noroviruses, Rotavirus A, Hepatitis A virus, Astrovirus MLB1, Human mastadenovirus F.
Interpretation and novelty
We also detected multiple Influenza virus subtypes, including Influenza A subtypes H1N1, H3N2, H5N1, H7N1, and H9N2, along with Influenza B and Influenza C viruses. Continuous surveillance can provide early warning signals to public health authorities, timely decision-making and coordinated responses can reduce the spread of disease which also requires collaboration with national and international health authorities.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that wastewater surveillance at airports can be a valuable tool for monitoring viral diversity and detecting emerging pathogens, which may inform public health strategies for disease prevention and control.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength for the study.

Contact behavior and serological evidence of influenza reinfection across three post-COVID-19 pandemic seasons in a nationwide German cohort.

Contact behavior and serological evidence of influenza reinfection across three post-COVID-19 pandemic seasons in a nationwide German cohort.
作者： Heiko Hampel, Manuela Harries, Carolina Judith Klett-Tammen, Patrick Marsall, Johanna Marie Griesbaum, Madeleine Fandrich, Alex Dulovic, Jana Prokein, André Karch, Veronika K. Jaeger
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Influenza remains a global public health burden. While interpersonal contacts drive transmission, their association with individual-level reinfection risk is not well quantified, particularly in the post-COVID-19 period.
设计与对象
本研究是一项多中心、基于人群的面板研究,于2020年至2024年间在德国全国性队列研究中进行。研究对象包括在三个连续季节中被评估流感再感染血清学证据的参与者。
核心方法
通过基于珠子的多重免疫测定法测量了针对三种流感亚型(A(H1N1)、甲型H3N2流感病毒和B)血凝抗原的结合抗体滴度。设计了逻辑回归模型,以估计与总接触、家庭接触和非家庭接触相关的流感再感染的粗略比值比(ORs)和基于DAG的调整比值比(aORs)。
主要结果
在参与者中,分别有14%(2021/22)、17%(2022/23)和12%(2023/24)检测到血清学再感染。在自述未接种疫苗的参与者中,再感染率分别为6%、13%和17%。
解释与创新
接触行为与再感染之间的关联具有季节性,家庭接触显示出最强且最一致的影响。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了关于接触行为在流感再感染中作用的见解,可以为有针对性的公共卫生干预措施和监测策略提供信息。
局限与证据强度
由于疫苗接种或先前感染的残留误分类,可能部分解释了2021/22赛季无关联的现象。由于血清学分类中可能存在偏倚和局限性,证据强度中等。

Contact behavior and serological evidence of influenza reinfection across three post-COVID-19 pandemic seasons in a nationwide German cohort.

Authors: Heiko Hampel, Manuela Harries, Carolina Judith Klett-Tammen, Patrick Marsall, Johanna Marie Griesbaum, Madeleine Fandrich, Alex Dulovic, Jana Prokein, André Karch, Veronika K. Jaeger
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Influenza remains a global public health burden. While interpersonal contacts drive transmission, their association with individual-level reinfection risk is not well quantified, particularly in the post-COVID-19 period. We assessed the relationship between contact frequency and serological evidence of influenza reinfection across three consecutive seasons. METHODS: In this multicentre, population-based panel study conducted between 2020 and 2024, binding antibody titers against hemagglutinin antigens of three influenza subtypes (A(H1N1), A(H3N2), and B) were measured by a bead-based multiplex immunoassay. Serological evidence of influenza reinfection was defined by fold-changes relative to the preceding season; with adjustments to minimize misclassification due to influenza vaccination. Logistic regression models were designed to estimate crude odds ratios (ORs) and DAG-informed adjusted odds ratios (aORs) for reinfection associated with total, household, and non-household contacts. RESULTS: Serological reinfection was detected in 14% (2021/22), 17% (2022/23), and 12% (2023/24) of participants. Among self-reported unvaccinated participants, reinfection was detected in 6%, 13% and 17% across the respective seasons. Non-household contacts were associated with increased odds of reinfection in one season (2022/23: aOR 1.008, 95% CI 1.002-1.014), while household contacts were associated with higher odds of reinfection in two seasons (2022/23: aOR 1.285, 95% CI 0.905-1.823; 2023/24: aOR 1.267, 95% CI 0.685-2.343). CONCLUSIONS: Serological evidence of reinfection among unvaccinated participants in comparison with all participants aligned more closely with surveillance data in 2021/22. Residual misclassification of vaccination or preceding infections may partly explain the absence of an association in 2021/22. Associations between contact behavior and reinfection were season-specific, with household contacts showing the strongest and most consistent effects.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Influenza remains a global public health burden. While interpersonal contacts drive transmission, their association with individual-level reinfection risk is not well quantified, particularly in the post-COVID-19 period.
Design and population
This multicentre, population-based panel study was conducted between 2020 and 2024 in a nationwide German cohort. It included participants who were assessed for serological evidence of influenza reinfection across three consecutive seasons.
Core methods
Binding antibody titers against hemagglutinin antigens of three influenza subtypes (A(H1N1), A(H3N2), and B) were measured by a bead-based multiplex immunoassay. Logistic regression models were designed to estimate crude odds ratios (ORs) and DAG-informed adjusted odds ratios (aORs) for reinfection associated with total, household, and non-household contacts.
Main results
Serological reinfection was detected in 14% (2021/22), 17% (2022/23), and 12% (2023/24) of participants. Among self-reported unvaccinated participants, reinfection was detected in 6%, 13%, and 17% across the respective seasons.
Interpretation and novelty
Associations between contact behavior and reinfection were season-specific, with household contacts showing the strongest and most consistent effects.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide insights into the role of contact behavior in influenza reinfection, which can inform targeted public health interventions and surveillance strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
Residual misclassification of vaccination or preceding infections may partly explain the absence of an association in 2021/22. The evidence is moderate due to potential biases and limitations in serological classification.

2023–2026年哈萨克斯坦监测期间鉴定的甲型H1N1流感病毒的分子遗传特征研究

Study of Molecular Genetic Characteristics of Influenza A(H1N1) Viruses Identified During Monitoring in Kazakhstan (2023–2026)
作者： Nailya Klivleyeva, Tatyana Glebova, Assem Baimukhametova, Kobey Karamendin, Nurbol Saktaganov, Nuray Ongarbayeva, Mereke Kalkozhayeva, Indira Ibragimova, Aknur S. Mutaliyeva, Altynay Gabiden
摘要中文翻译背景/目的:季节性流感通过快速突变威胁全球公共卫生。本研究评估了2023–2026年流行季节期间在哈萨克斯坦流行的季节性流感病毒的患病率和分子遗传特征。方法:通过RT-qPCR、HI和ELISA检测方法对1756份鼻咽拭子样本和718份血清样本进行了筛查。选取的甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09分离株进行了全长HA和NA基因的下一代测序,随后进行了系统发育和表型分析。结果:2023–2024和2025–2026流行季节期间,哈萨克斯坦主要流行的是流感甲型H3N2流感病毒毒株。相反,2024–2025流行季节呈现出变化趋势,表现为流感B毒株与属于遗传簇6B.1A.5a.2a(亚簇C.1.9和C.1.9.3)的甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09毒株共同流行。突变图谱显示,在受体结合位点(HA-R222K)附近持续发生局部微进化。对NA基因的基因组筛查发现了传统上与抗药性相关的I223R和S247N 突变突变。计算机模拟分析预测,尽管大多数毒株保持基础敏感性,但单独携带I223R突变的A/Shymkent/NRL-1999/2024毒株对奥司他韦、拉米夫定和扎那米韦表现出多重耐药基因型。结论:这些发现表明了不同流行季节间病原体的动态变化,并预测了独立耐药谱系(NA-I223R)的出现。这强调了在哈萨克斯坦对呼吸道病原体进行持续、扩展的基因组监测监测的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
抗病毒药物通过快速抗原漂移威胁全球公共卫生。本研究评估了2023–2026年流行季节期间在哈萨克斯坦循环的甲型H1N1流感病毒的分子遗传特征。
设计与对象
本研究在哈萨克斯坦2023–2026年流行季节期间进行,涉及从疑似流感感染患者中收集的1756份鼻咽拭子和718份血清样本。
核心方法
鼻咽拭子和血清样本通过RT-qPCR、HI和ELISA检测进行了筛查。所选甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09分离株进行了全长HA和NA基因的下一代测序,随后进行了系统发育和表型分析。
主要结果
2023–2024和2025–2026季节期间,甲型H3N2流感病毒型流感病毒在哈萨克斯坦占主导地位。相反,2024–2025季节显示出变化的流行趋势,表现为流感B病毒与属于遗传簇6B.1A.5a.2a(亚簇C.1.9和C.1.9.3)的甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09型流感病毒共同流行。突变图谱显示,在受体结合位点(HA-R222K)附近持续发生局部微进化。对NA基因的基因组筛查发现了与抗性相关的关键I223R和S247N 突变突变。
解释与创新
这些发现表明了季节间病原体流行特征的动态变化,并预测了独立耐药谱系(NA-I223R)的出现。
科研与公卫意义
这强调了在哈萨克斯坦对呼吸道病原体进行持续、扩展的基因组监测的必要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅基于摘要证据,可能缺乏详细的上下文或方法信息。由于依赖计算机模拟分析且缺乏临床结果数据,证据强度中等。

Study of Molecular Genetic Characteristics of Influenza A(H1N1) Viruses Identified During Monitoring in Kazakhstan (2023–2026)

Authors: Nailya Klivleyeva, Tatyana Glebova, Assem Baimukhametova, Kobey Karamendin, Nurbol Saktaganov, Nuray Ongarbayeva, Mereke Kalkozhayeva, Indira Ibragimova, Aknur S. Mutaliyeva, Altynay Gabiden
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objectives: Seasonal influenza threatens global public health through rapid mutations. This study evaluated the prevalence and molecular-genetic characteristics of seasonal influenza viruses circulating in Kazakhstan during the 2023–2026 epidemic seasons. Methods: Nasopharyngeal swabs (n = 1756) and serum samples (n = 718) were screened using RT-qPCR, HI, and ELISA assays. Selected A(H1N1)pdm09 isolates underwent full-length HA and NA gene next-generation sequencing, followed by phylogenetic and phenotypic analyses. Results: Influenza A(H3N2) strains dominated in Kazakhstan during the 2023–2024 and 2025–2026 seasons. Conversely, the 2024–2025 season showed a shifting landscape, characterized by co-circulation of influenza B and A(H1N1)pdm09 strains belonging to genetic clade 6B.1A.5a.2a (subclades C.1.9 and C.1.9.3). Mutational mapping revealed ongoing in situ microevolution adjacent to the receptor-binding site (HA-R222K). Genomic screening of the NA gene identified critical I223R and S247N mutations traditionally linked to resistance. In silico profiling predicted that while the majority maintained baseline sensitivity, the solitary A/Shymkent/NRL-1999/2024 isolate harboring the I223R substitution exhibited a multidrug-resistant genotype to oseltamivir, peramivir, and zanamivir. Conclusions: These findings demonstrate dynamic inter-seasonal etiological shifts and the predicted emergence of an independent resistant lineage (NA-I223R). This underscores the necessity for continuous, expanded genomic surveillance of respiratory pathogens across Kazakhstan.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Seasonal influenza threatens global public health through rapid mutations. This study evaluated the prevalence and molecular-genetic characteristics of seasonal influenza viruses circulating in Kazakhstan during the 2023–2026 epidemic seasons.
Design and population
The study was conducted in Kazakhstan during the 2023–2026 epidemic seasons, involving 1756 nasopharyngeal swabs and 718 serum samples collected from patients suspected of influenza infection.
Core methods
Nasopharyngeal swabs and serum samples were screened using RT-qPCR, HI, and ELISA assays. Selected A(H1N1)pdm09 isolates underwent full-length HA and NA gene next-generation sequencing, followed by phylogenetic and phenotypic analyses.
Main results
Influenza A(H3N2) strains dominated in Kazakhstan during the 2023–2024 and 2025–2026 seasons. Conversely, the 2024–2025 season showed a shifting landscape, characterized by co-circulation of influenza B and A(H1N1)pdm09 strains belonging to genetic clade 6B.1A.5a.2a (subclades C.1.9 and C.1.9.3). Mutational mapping revealed ongoing in situ microevolution adjacent to the receptor-binding site (HA-R222K). Genomic screening of the NA gene identified critical I223R and S247N mutations traditionally linked to resistance.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings demonstrate dynamic inter-seasonal etiological shifts and the predicted emergence of an independent resistant lineage (NA-I223R).
Scientific and public-health significance
This underscores the necessity for continuous, expanded genomic surveillance of respiratory pathogens across Kazakhstan.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, which may lack detailed contextual or methodological information. The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on in silico profiling and the absence of clinical outcome data.

重组和细胞培养基流感疫苗的比较免疫原性和相对有效性:2024-2025年美国成年人随机试验结果

Comparative immunogenicity and relative vaccine efficacy of recombinant and cell culture-based influenza vaccines: Results from a randomized trial of adults in the United States, 2024-2025
作者： Kelsey M. Sumner, Emma K. Noble, Lauren Grant, Jessica Meeker, Vel Murugan, Lora Nordstrom, Stacey L House, Rachel Presti, Emmanuel B. Walter, Olivia L. Williams
摘要中文翻译背景:重组(RIV)和基于细胞培养的灭活(ccIIV)流感疫苗可能改善流感预防;然而,关于非蛋基流感疫苗保护效果差异的证据有限。方法:我们进行了一项随机对照试验,比较RIV与ccIIV在2024-25流感季节18至64岁成人中的免疫原性(ClinicalTrials.gov,NCT06518577)。在接种疫苗后六个月,对流感样疾病的主动监测,并比较不同流感病毒类型引起的流感累积发病率,以估计相对疫苗效力。参与者通过逆转录聚合酶链反应或快速抗原检测进行流感诊断。免疫原性通过血凝抑制(HAI)、病毒微中和(MN)和神经氨酸酶抑制(NAI)试验在部分接种前和接种后一个月采集的血液样本中进行评估。结果:在591名参与者中,检测到25例流感A型病例;RIV接种者的累积发病率为2.4%,而ccIIV接种者为6.1%。RIV相较于ccIIV降低流感发生概率61%(95%置信区间:7至84)。在151名参与者中,接种后RIV接种者的血凝抑制和中和抗体滴度高于ccIIV接种者,而神经氨酸酶抑制滴度则更高。结论:在2024-25流感季节,RIV在成人中对流感A型疾病的保护效果优于ccIIV。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
重组(RIV)和细胞培养基灭活(ccIIV)流感疫苗可能改善流感预防;然而,关于非蛋基流感疫苗之间保护效果差异的证据有限。
设计与对象
我们开展了一项RIV与ccIIV的随机试验,比较2024-25流感季节中18至64岁成年人的疫苗免疫原性。
核心方法
参与者通过逆转录聚合酶链反应或快速抗原检测进行流感诊断。
主要结果
在591名参与者中,检测到25例流感A型病例;RIV接种者的累积感染率为2.4%,而ccIIV接种者为6.1%。
解释与创新
在2024-25流感季节期间,RIV在成年人中对流感A型疾病提供了比ccIIV更好的保护。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,RIV可能在成年人中提供对流感A型疾病的更好保护,这可能有助于制定针对流感预防的疫苗选择策略。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告该研究的局限性。

Comparative immunogenicity and relative vaccine efficacy of recombinant and cell culture-based influenza vaccines: Results from a randomized trial of adults in the United States, 2024-2025

Authors: Kelsey M. Sumner, Emma K. Noble, Lauren Grant, Jessica Meeker, Vel Murugan, Lora Nordstrom, Stacey L House, Rachel Presti, Emmanuel B. Walter, Olivia L. Williams
Original abstract (English)
Background: Recombinant (RIV) and cell culture-based inactivated (ccIIV) influenza vaccines could improve seasonal influenza prevention; however, evidence is limited in how protection may differ among non-egg-based vaccines. Methods: We conducted a randomized trial of RIV versus ccIIV to compare vaccine immunogenicity in adults aged 18 to 64 years during the 2024-25 influenza season (ClinicalTrials.gov, NCT06518577 ). Active surveillance for influenza-like illness was conducted for six months post-vaccination and cumulative incidence of influenza by vaccine type was compared to estimate relative vaccine efficacy. Participants were tested for influenza by reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction or rapid antigen tests. Immunogenicity was evaluated by hemagglutination inhibition (HAI), virus microneutralization (MN), and neuraminidase inhibition (NAI) assays in a subset of blood specimens collected pre-vaccination and one-month post-vaccination. Results: Among the 591 participants, 25 cases of influenza A were detected; 2.4% cumulative incidence among RIV recipients versus 6.1% among ccIIV recipients. The probability of influenza was 61% lower with RIV than ccIIV (95% confidence interval: 7 to 84). Among 151 participants, post-vaccination HAI and MN titers vaccine components were higher among RIV recipients while NAI titers were higher among ccIIV recipients. Conclusions: RIV provided better protection than ccIIV against influenza A illness in adults during the U.S.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Recombinant (RIV) and cell culture-based inactivated (ccIIV) influenza vaccines could improve seasonal influenza prevention; however, evidence is limited in how protection may differ among non-egg-based vaccines.
Design and population
We conducted a randomized trial of RIV versus ccIIV to compare vaccine immunogenicity in adults aged 18 to 64 years during the 2024-25 influenza season.
Core methods
Participants were tested for influenza by reverse-transcription polymerase chain reaction or rapid antigen tests.
Main results
Among the 591 participants, 25 cases of influenza A were detected; 2.4% cumulative incidence among RIV recipients versus 6.1% among ccIIV recipients.
Interpretation and novelty
RIV provided better protection than ccIIV against influenza A illness in adults during the U.S.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that RIV may offer improved protection against influenza A illness in adults, which could inform vaccine selection strategies for seasonal influenza prevention.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations of the study.

2022-2026年韩国流感A流行后流感B季节性主导的反复时间关联性

Recurring Temporal Association Between Influenza A Epidemics and Subsequent Influenza B Dominance in Post-Pandemic Korea, 2022-2026.
作者： Yoon Lee, Yoonsun Yoon, Ji Eun Park, Won Keun Kim, Youn Young Choi, Yun-Kyung Kim, Changmin Kang, Young Yoo, Young June Choe
摘要中文翻译后疫情时代流感的动态变化在全球范围内表现出异常模式。我们研究了韩国在三个后疫情时代季节(2022-2026)中流感B病毒的重新出现及其反复的季节性主导现象。我们分析了157周连续的国家哨兵监测数据(K-SenS,韩国疾病控制与预防署),涵盖2022年9月至2025年8月,以及2026年第10周的初步数据。将流感B病毒活动占比超过50%的周视为流感B主导;敏感性分析要求总体流感活动强度超过5%。在2022-23季节,流感B活动水平保持较低(平均占比3.3%;峰值25.3%),从未达到主导状态。在两次流感A流行之后出现了完整的流感B主导期:在2023-24季节(同时流行A/H1N1pdm09和A/H3N2;ILI峰值61.3/1000)之后,流感B主导始于2024年1月并持续了23周(峰值100%);在2024-25季节以A/H1N1pdm09为主导的流行(ILI峰值99.8/1000)之后,流感B主导始于2025年2月,并在两个时期累计持续了20周,中间有一次短暂中断(峰值100%)。在所有三个案例中,流感B主导的开始时间均在流感A峰值后5-8周(病毒学上为6-8周),且不受阈值选择的影响。在2025-26季节以A/H3N2为主导的流行之后,流感B主导现象仍在持续(截至2026年3月),其开始时间为流感A峰值后7周。在后疫情时代的韩国,流感B病毒在三个连续季节中反复在流感A流行后5-8周建立季节性主导。这种反复的时间模式可能对流感监测、临床准备以及疫苗策略产生影响,尽管其潜在机制尚未明确。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
后疫情时代流感的动态变化在全球范围内表现出异常模式。我们研究了韩国在三个后疫情时代季节(2022-2026)中流感B病毒的重新出现及其反复的季节性主导现象。
设计与对象
本研究分析了157周连续的国家哨兵监测数据(K-SenS,韩国疾病控制与预防署),涵盖2022年9月至2025年8月,以及2026年第10周的初步数据。研究聚焦于韩国在三个后疫情时代季节中流感B病毒的主导情况。在2022-23季节,流感B活动水平保持较低,从未达到主导状态。
核心方法
本研究利用国家哨兵监测数据分析流感活动,将流感B病毒活动占比超过50%的周定义为流感B主导。敏感性分析要求总体流感活动强度超过5%。
主要结果
在两次流感A流行之后出现了完整的流感B主导期:在2023-24季节(同时流行A/H1N1pdm09和A/H3N2;ILI峰值61.3/1000)之后,流感B主导始于2024年1月并持续了23周(峰值100%);在2024-25季节以A/H1N1pdm09为主导的流行(ILI峰值99.8/1000)之后,流感B主导始于2025年2月,并在两个时期累计持续了20周,中间有一次短暂中断(峰值100%)。
解释与创新
本研究观察到,在三个连续季节中,流感B病毒在流感A流行后5-8周反复建立季节性主导。这种反复的时间模式可能对流感监测、临床准备以及疫苗策略产生影响。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为后疫情时代韩国的流感监测策略、临床准备和疫苗规划提供信息,强调了监测流感A和B主导之间时间关系的必要性。
局限与证据强度
本研究基于监测数据,未能确定所观察模式的潜在机制。由于依赖观察性数据而非实验验证,证据强度中等。

Recurring Temporal Association Between Influenza A Epidemics and Subsequent Influenza B Dominance in Post-Pandemic Korea, 2022-2026.

Authors: Yoon Lee, Yoonsun Yoon, Ji Eun Park, Won Keun Kim, Youn Young Choi, Yun-Kyung Kim, Changmin Kang, Young Yoo, Young June Choe
Original abstract (English)
Post-pandemic influenza dynamics have shown unusual patterns globally. We investigated the re-emergence and recurrent seasonal dominance of influenza B in Korea across three post-pandemic seasons (2022-2026). We analyzed 157 consecutive weeks of national sentinel surveillance data (K-SenS, Korea Disease Control and Prevention Agency), covering September 2022 to August 2025, plus preliminary data through Week 10 of 2026. Influenza B predominance was defined as weeks with > 50% influenza B among total influenza detections; a sensitivity analysis required overall influenza activity > 5%. Influenza B activity remained minimal during the 2022-23 season (mean proportion 3.3%; peak 25.3%) and never reached predominance. Two complete predominance episodes followed influenza A epidemics: after the 2023-24 epidemic (co-circulating A/H1N1pdm09 and A/H3N2; peak ILI 61.3/1000), B predominance began January 2024 and persisted 23 weeks (peak 100%); after the 2024-25A/H1N1pdm09-dominant epidemic (peak ILI 99.8/1000), it began February 2025 and accumulated 20 weeks over two periods with a brief interruption (peak 100%). Onset occurred 5-8 weeks after the influenza A peak by ILI (6-8 weeks virologically) in all three episodes, unaffected by threshold choice. A third episode following the 2025-26A/H3N2 epidemic was ongoing as of March 2026 (onset 7 weeks after the A peak). In post-pandemic Korea, influenza B repeatedly established seasonal dominance 5-8 weeks after influenza A epidemics across three consecutive seasons. This recurring temporal pattern may have implications for influenza surveillance, clinical preparedness, and vaccine strategy, though its underlying mechanism remains unestablished.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Post-pandemic influenza dynamics have shown unusual patterns globally. We investigated the re-emergence and recurrent seasonal dominance of influenza B in Korea across three post-pandemic seasons (2022-2026).
Design and population
The study analyzed 157 consecutive weeks of national sentinel surveillance data (K-SenS, Korea Disease Control and Prevention Agency), covering September 2022 to August 2025, plus preliminary data through Week 10 of 2026. The study focused on influenza B dominance in Korea during three post-pandemic seasons. Influenza B activity remained minimal during the 2022-23 season and never reached predominance.
Core methods
The study used national sentinel surveillance data to analyze influenza activity, defining influenza B predominance as weeks with > 50% influenza B among total influenza detections. A sensitivity analysis required overall influenza activity > 5%.
Main results
Two complete predominance episodes followed influenza A epidemics: after the 2023-24 epidemic (co-circulating A/H1N1pdm09 and A/H3N2; peak ILI 61.3/1000), B predominance began January 2024 and persisted 23 weeks (peak 100%); after the 2024-25 A/H1N1pdm09-dominant epidemic (peak ILI 99.8/1000), it began February 2025 and accumulated 20 weeks over two periods with a brief interruption (peak 100%).
Interpretation and novelty
The study observed that influenza B repeatedly established seasonal dominance 5-8 weeks after influenza A epidemics across three consecutive seasons. This recurring temporal pattern may have implications for influenza surveillance, clinical preparedness, and vaccine strategy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform influenza surveillance strategies, clinical preparedness, and vaccine planning in post-pandemic Korea, highlighting the need for monitoring the temporal relationship between influenza A and B dominance.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on surveillance data and does not establish the underlying mechanism of the observed pattern. The evidence is moderate due to the reliance on observational data without experimental validation.

党参多糖的结构表征及其抗病毒药物活性研究。

Structural characterization and antiviral activity of polysaccharide from Codonopsis pilosula.
作者： Ya-hui Song, Fang-qin Mao, Liu MF, Zhang-quan Liu, Qing-yan Xin, Lan Yu
摘要中文翻译党参多糖表现出多种生物活性,但其对甲型H1N1流感病毒的抗病毒药物作用及其在硫酸化修饰后活性的变化仍不清楚。本研究通过水提醇沉法获得粗党参多糖(COP),并分离纯化出COP-1(2.1 × 103 Da,β-D-(2 → 1)-果聚糖)。采用三氧化硫-吡啶法对COP和COP-1进行硫酸化修饰,得到取代度分别为1.792和1.403的衍生物SCOP和SCOP-1。通过MTT和TCID50实验在MDCK细胞中评估抗病毒药物活性,结果显示COP、SCOP和SCOP-1均抑制了H1N1病毒的复制,硫酸化显著增强了抗病毒药物活性。时间添加实验表明,抑制作用发生在感染早期阶段(0-4小时)。通过Annexin V-PI染色确认这些化合物可减少病毒诱导的早期细胞凋亡。在H1N1感染的A549细胞中,它们抑制了NF-κB p65的磷酸化,并下调了IL-6、IL-8、MCP-1、CCL-5、TNF-α和TRAIL的表达。总体而言,党参多糖及其硫酸化衍生物通过抑制早期病毒复制、抗凋亡作用和抗炎作用三种机制发挥抗流感效果,显示出作为抗流感药物的潜力。

问题与背景
党参多糖表现出多种生物活性,但其对甲型H1N1流感病毒的抗病毒药物作用及其在硫酸化修饰后活性的变化仍不清楚。

Structural characterization and antiviral activity of polysaccharide from Codonopsis pilosula.

Authors: Ya-hui Song, Fang-qin Mao, Liu MF, Zhang-quan Liu, Qing-yan Xin, Lan Yu
Original abstract (English)
Codonopsis polysaccharides exhibit diverse biological activities, but their antiviral effects against the H1N1 influenza A virus and how these activities change after sulfation modification remains unclear. In this study, crude Codonopsis polysaccharide (COP) was obtained via water extraction and alcohol precipitation, and COP-1 (2.1 × 103 Da, β-D-(2 → 1)-fructan) was isolated and purified. Both COP and COP-1 were modified by sulfation using the sulfur trioxide-pyridine method, yielding derivatives SCOP and SCOP-1 with degrees of substitution of 1.792 and 1.403, respectively. Antiviral activity was evaluated in MDCK cells using MTT and TCID50 assays, revealing that COP, SCOP, and SCOP-1 all inhibited H1N1 replication, with sulfation significantly enhancing antiviral activity. Time-of-addition experiments indicated that inhibition occurred during the early phase of infection (0-4 h). Annexin V-PI staining confirmed that these compounds reduced virus-induced early apoptosis. In H1N1-infected A549 cells, they suppressed NF-κB p65 phosphorylation and downregulated the expression of IL-6, IL-8, MCP-1, CCL-5, TNF-α, and TRAIL. Overall, Codonopsis polysaccharides and their sulfated derivatives exert anti-influenza effects through a triple mechanism involving inhibition of early viral replication, anti-apoptotic activity, and anti-inflammatory action, making them promising candidates for antiflu therapeutics.

Question and background
Codonopsis polysaccharides exhibit diverse biological activities, but their antiviral effects against the H1N1 influenza A virus and how these activities change after sulfation modification remains unclear.
Design and population
In this study, crude Codonopsis polysaccharide (COP) was obtained via water extraction and alcohol precipitation, and COP-1 (2.1 × 103 Da, β-D-(2 → 1)-fructan) was isolated and purified.

Main results

Scientific and public-health significance

Limitations and evidence strength

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

急性呼吸道病原体变化:一项在中国杭州(2018–2025)进行的8年研究

Shifts in acute respiratory pathogen epidemiology: an 8-year surveillance study in Hangzhou, China (2018–2025)
作者： Yi Wang, 霍亮亮, Jia-Yun Wang, Xin-Ye Jin, 蒋龙芳, L. Huo, Long-Fang Jiang
摘要中文翻译目的 分析2018年至2025年间杭州市急性呼吸道感染(ARTI)病例中六种常见呼吸道病原体的流行病学特征,为临床诊断和治疗以及公共卫生预防和控制提供依据。方法 开展了多中心回顾性研究。收集了2018年1月至2025年12月期间杭州市7家医疗机构的81,974例ARTI病例数据。对流感A病毒(FluA)、流感B病毒(FluB)、呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)、腺病毒(ADV)、肺炎支原体(MP)和肺炎衣原体(CP)的阳性病例进行分析,并根据疫情阶段、年龄、性别、季节和混合感染状态进行统计分析。结果 在81,974例患者中,共检测出33,350例病原体阳性病例,总体阳性率为40.68%。MP(19.56%)和FluA(16.93%)是最常见的病原体。每种病原体的阳性率在新冠疫情(COVID-19)爆发前、爆发期间和爆发后三个阶段均表现出统计学显著差异(所有P < 0.001)。与新冠疫情爆发前(2018–2019年)相比,新冠疫情爆发期间(2020–2022年)FluA、FluB和MP的阳性率显著下降,而在新冠疫情爆发后(2023–2025年)则出现了明显的‘报复性反弹’(例如,MP的阳性率从5.43%反弹至21.09%)。相比之下,RSV、ADV和CP的阳性率表现出持续抑制状态,但病例的绝对数量远高于新冠疫情爆发前。MP在6–<12岁人群中感染率最高(38.31%),而FluA在成人中更为常见。流行病学分布显示出明显的季节性差异:FluA/FluB/RSV在冬季更为常见,MP在秋季活跃,ADV则在夏季更为普遍。混合感染率为7.22%(5,922例),最常见的组合是FluA + MP,占所有混合感染的53.82%。结论 2018年至2025年间,杭州市ARTI的主要病原体谱系由MP和FluA组成。新冠疫情及其相关的非药物干预措施(NPIs)对呼吸道病原体的流行模式产生了深远且异质性的影响。FluA、FluB和MP表现出‘抑制-报复性反弹’的特征。在新冠疫情后时期,病原体的活动呈现出明显的季节性、年龄和性别分布模式,混合感染率较高。研究结果表明,需要建立动态监测系统,并在高发病率季节对关键人群和病原体实施针对性的预防和控制措施。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在分析2018年至2025年间杭州急性呼吸道感染(ARTI)病例中六种常见呼吸道病原体的流行病学特征,为临床诊断和治疗以及公共卫生预防和控制提供依据。
设计与对象
本研究采用多中心回顾性研究设计,使用了2018年1月至2025年12月期间杭州七家医疗机构收集的81,974例急性呼吸道感染(ARTI)病例数据。
核心方法
收集了2018年1月至2025年12月期间杭州七家医疗机构报告的81,974例急性呼吸道感染(ARTI)病例数据。对流感A病毒(FluA)、流感B病毒(FluB)、呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)、腺病毒(ADV)、肺炎支原体(MP)和肺炎衣原体(CP)的阳性病例进行分析,并根据疫情阶段、年龄、性别、季节和混合感染状态进行统计分析。
主要结果
在81,974名患者中,共有33,350例检测出病原体,总体阳性率为40.68%。每种病原体的阳性率在新冠疫情(Coronavirus Disease 2019,COVID-19)大流行前、期间和后三个阶段中均表现出统计学上的显著差异(所有P < 0.001)。与新冠疫情前(2018–2019年)相比,新冠疫情大流行期间(2020–2022年)流感A型(FluA)、流感B型(FluB)和支原体(MP)的阳性率显著下降,随后在新冠疫情后(2023–2025年)出现了急剧的"报复性反弹"(例如,支原体的阳性率从5.43%反弹至21.09%)。
解释与创新
研究发现,在疫情和疫情后时期,流感A病毒、流感B病毒和肺炎支原体表现出“抑制-报复性反弹”的特征,具有明显的季节性、年龄和性别分布模式,并且混合感染率较高。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要建立动态监测系统,并在高发季节对关键人群和病原体实施针对性的预防和控制措施。
局限与证据强度
本研究是一项回顾性分析,可能会引入偏倚并限制建立因果关系的能力。研究证据基于大规模数据集,结果通过统计分析支持,表明具有中等程度的可信度。

Shifts in acute respiratory pathogen epidemiology: an 8-year surveillance study in Hangzhou, China (2018–2025)

Authors: Yi Wang, 霍亮亮, Jia-Yun Wang, Xin-Ye Jin, 蒋龙芳, L. Huo, Long-Fang Jiang
Original abstract (English)
Objectives To analyze the epidemiological characteristics of six common respiratory pathogens in cases of Acute Respiratory Tract Infection (ARTI) in Hangzhou from 2018 to 2025, to provide a basis for clinical diagnosis and treatment, as well as public health prevention and control. Methods A multicenter retrospective study was conducted. Data from 81,974 ARTI cases from seven medical institutions in Hangzhou City between January 2018 and December 2025 were collected. Cases with positive test results were analyzed for influenza A virus (FluA), influenza B virus (FluB), respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), adenovirus (ADV), Mycoplasma pneumoniae (MP), and Chlamydia pneumoniae (CP), and statistical analysis was performed according to the pandemic stage, age, sex, season, and co-infection status. Results Among 81,974 patients, a total of 33,350 cases tested positive for pathogens, with an overall positive rate of 40.68%. MP (19.56%) and FluA (16.93%) were the most prevalent pathogens. The positivity rates of each pathogen showed statistically significant differences across the three stages before, during, and after the Coronavirus Disease 2019 (COVID-19) pandemic (all P < 0.001). Compared with the pre-COVID-19 pandemic period (2018–2019), the positive rates of FluA, FluB, and MP were significantly suppressed during the COVID-19 pandemic period (2020–2022), and then experienced a sharp “revenge rebound” in the post-COVID-19 pandemic period (2023–2025) (for example, the positive rate of MP rebounded from 5.43% to 21.09%). In contrast, the positive rates of RSV, ADV, and CP showed a persistent state of inhibition, but the absolute number of cases far exceeded that of before the COVID-19 pandemic. MP had the highest infection rate among 6– < 12 years old (38.31%), whereas FluA was more common in adults. The epidemiological distribution showed obvious seasonal heterogeneity: FluA/FluB/RSV were more prevalent in winter, MP was active in autumn, and ADV was more prevalent in summer. The co-infection rate was 7.22% (5,922 cases), and the most common combination was FluA + MP, accounting for 53.82% of all co-infections. Conclusion From 2018 to 2025, the pathogen spectrum of ARTI in Hangzhou was mainly composed of MP and FluA. The COVID-19 pandemic and related non-pharmaceutical interventions (NPIs) have had a profound and heterogeneous impact on the epidemic pattern of respiratory pathogens. FluA, FluB, and MP showed the characteristics of “suppression-retaliatory rebound.” In the post-COVID-19 pandemic era, the activity of pathogens has clear seasonal, age, and gender distribution patterns, and the rate of mixed infections is high. The research results suggest that a dynamic monitoring system needs to be established, and targeted prevention and control measures should be implemented for key individuals and pathogens during high-incidence seasons.

Question and background
The study aimed to analyze the epidemiological characteristics of six common respiratory pathogens in cases of Acute Respiratory Tract Infection (ARTI) in Hangzhou from 2018 to 2025, to provide a basis for clinical diagnosis and treatment, as well as public health prevention and control.
Design and population
A multicenter retrospective study was conducted using data from 81,974 ARTI cases collected from seven medical institutions in Hangzhou City between January 2018 and December 2025.
Core methods
Data from 81,974 ARTI cases from seven medical institutions in Hangzhou City between January 2018 and December 2025 were collected. Cases with positive test results were analyzed for influenza A virus (FluA), influenza B virus (FluB), respiratory syncytial virus (RSV), adenovirus (ADV), Mycoplasma pneumoniae (MP), and Chlamydia pneumoniae (CP), and statistical analysis was performed according to the pandemic stage, age, sex, season, and co-infection status.
Main results
Among 81,974 patients, a total of 33,350 cases tested positive for pathogens, with an overall positive rate of 40.68%. The positivity rates of each pathogen showed statistically significant differences across the three stages before, during, and after the Coronavirus Disease 2019 (COVID-19) pandemic (all P < 0.001). Compared with the pre-COVID-19 pandemic period (2018–2019), the positive rates of FluA, FluB, and MP were significantly suppressed during the COVID-19 pandemic period (2020–2022), and then experienced a sharp "revenge rebound" in the post-COVID-19 pandemic period (2023–2025) (for example, the positive rate of MP rebounded from 5.43% to 21.09%).
Interpretation and novelty
The study found that FluA, FluB, and MP showed the characteristics of "suppression-retaliatory rebound" during the pandemic and post-pandemic periods, with clear seasonal, age, and gender distribution patterns and a high rate of mixed infections.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that a dynamic monitoring system needs to be established, and targeted prevention and control measures should be implemented for key individuals and pathogens during high-incidence seasons.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is a retrospective analysis, which may introduce biases and limit the ability to establish causality. The evidence is based on a large dataset, and the results are supported by statistical analysis, indicating a moderate level of confidence.

受体结合几何结构通过适应性逃逸耦合限制病毒抗原性进化.

Receptor-binding geometry constrains viral antigenic evolution through fitness-evasion coupling.
作者： Wentai Ma, Xuan Jiang, Mingkun Li
摘要中文翻译尽管病毒抗原性进化受到免疫逃逸和受体结合适应性的竞争需求的影响,但不同病毒进化动态背后的物理原理仍不明确。在这里,通过整合结构生物学、深度突变扫描和基因组监测数据,我们展示了受体结合几何结构如何限制抗体逃逸与内在病毒适应性之间的空间耦合。在SARS-CoV-2中,暴露的凸面刺突-ACE2界面与主要中和表位在空间上重叠,受体接触残基,从而产生严格的适应性逃逸权衡。因此,可行的逃逸突变主要局限于受体结合核心周围的狭窄外围区域。随着群体免疫逐渐填满这一有限的逃逸空间,持续适应越来越需要协调的受体结合界面突变重塑。相比之下,流感H3N2血凝素通过一个凹陷的口袋结构结合小的唾液酸配体,这种结构部分将主要抗体压力与受体结合适应性约束分离。这种拓扑解耦允许连续的逐步抗原漂移,而无需频繁重塑核心结合位点。基于这些发现,我们开发了一个轻量级的几何框架,能够准确识别不同病毒中的受体竞争性抗原热点。我们的结果共同支持了一个普遍模型,即受体几何结构和蛋白质表面拓扑结构强烈偏导可访问的抗原进化轨迹。重要性:为什么一些病毒通过渐进的抗原漂移进化,而另一些病毒则经历反复的进化跃迁,仍然是病毒学中的一个基本问题。通过整合蛋白质结构、突变扫描数据和基因组监测,我们展示了受体结合界面的几何结构如何影响免疫逃逸突变对病毒适应性的影响。在SARS-CoV-2中,受体结合残基高度暴露,并与主要抗体靶点重叠,限制了免疫逃逸突变的范围,并在该空间耗尽时促进周期性的进化跃迁。相比之下,流感A病毒H3N2的受体结合口袋部分被抗体识别所屏蔽,允许较少适应性约束下的连续抗原漂移。我们进一步证明,这些结构原理可用于直接从病毒-受体复合物中识别抗原热点。这些发现为病毒不同的进化轨迹提供了机制性解释,并为研究和预测抗原性进化提供了结构框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在理解为什么一些病毒通过渐进的抗原漂移进化,而另一些病毒则经历反复的进化跃迁,以解决病毒学中的一个基本问题。
设计与对象
本研究整合了结构生物学、深度突变扫描和基因组监测数据,以分析SARS-CoV-2和流感甲型H3N2流感病毒病毒的受体结合几何结构。
核心方法
研究人员结合蛋白质结构分析、突变扫描和基因组数据,以研究病毒进化中抗体逃逸与受体结合适应性之间的空间关系。
主要结果
研究结果表明,在SARS-CoV-2中,受体结合界面与主要抗体靶点在空间上重叠,限制了逃逸突变的范围,并在该空间耗尽时促进周期性的进化跃迁。相比之下,流感甲型H3N2流感病毒具有一个凹陷的口袋,该结构将抗体压力与受体结合适应性约束部分分离。
解释与创新
作者认为,受体几何结构和蛋白质表面拓扑结构强烈影响病毒可访问的进化轨迹,为理解抗原性进化提供了一个新颖的结构框架。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为预测和监测季节性流感病毒中的抗原性进化提供了机制基础,可以用于指导疫苗的开发和公共卫生策略。
局限与证据强度
本研究仅基于抽象层面的证据,结论基于来自多个来源的整合数据,支持中等置信度的评估。

Receptor-binding geometry constrains viral antigenic evolution through fitness-evasion coupling.

Authors: Wentai Ma, Xuan Jiang, Mingkun Li
Original abstract (English)
UNLABELLED: Although viral antigenic evolution is shaped by the competing demands of immune evasion and receptor-binding fitness, the physical principles underlying divergent viral evolutionary dynamics remain poorly understood. Here, by integrating structural biology, deep mutational scanning, and genomic surveillance data, we show that receptor-binding geometry constrains the spatial coupling between antibody escape and intrinsic viral fitness. In SARS-CoV-2, the exposed convex Spike-ACE2 interface spatially overlaps dominant neutralizing epitopes with receptor-contact residues, producing a stringent fitness-evasion trade-off. Consequently, viable escape mutations are largely restricted to a narrow peripheral region surrounding the receptor-binding core. As population immunity progressively saturates this limited escape space, continued adaptation increasingly requires coordinated saltational remodeling of the receptor-binding interface. In contrast, the influenza H3N2 hemagglutinin binds the small sialic acid ligand through a recessed pocket architecture that partially separates dominant antibody pressure from receptor-binding fitness constraints. This topological decoupling permits continuous stepwise antigenic drift without requiring frequent remodeling of the core binding site. Building on these findings, we develop a lightweight geometry-based framework that accurately identifies receptor-competitive antigenic hotspots across divergent viruses. Together, our results support a general model in which receptor geometry and protein surface topology strongly bias accessible antigenic evolutionary trajectories. IMPORTANCE: Why some viruses evolve through gradual antigenic drift whereas others undergo recurrent evolutionary leaps remains a fundamental question in virology. By integrating protein structures, mutational scanning data, and genomic surveillance, we show that the geometry of the receptor-binding interface strongly influences how immune escape mutations affect viral fitness. In SARS-CoV-2, receptor-binding residues are highly exposed and overlap with dominant antibody targets, restricting immune escape to a narrow set of mutations and promoting periodic evolutionary leaps when this space becomes exhausted. In contrast, the receptor-binding pocket of influenza A virus H3N2 is partially shielded from antibody recognition, allowing continuous antigenic drift with fewer fitness constraints. We further demonstrate that these structural principles can be used to identify antigenic hotspots directly from virus-receptor complexes. These findings provide a mechanistic explanation for divergent viral evolutionary trajectories and offer a structural framework for studying and anticipating antigenic evolution.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to understand why some viruses evolve through gradual antigenic drift while others undergo recurrent evolutionary leaps, addressing a fundamental question in virology.
Design and population
The study integrated structural biology, deep mutational scanning, and genomic surveillance data to analyze the receptor-binding geometry of SARS-CoV-2 and influenza A(H3N2) viruses.
Core methods
The researchers combined protein structure analysis, mutational scanning, and genomic data to examine the spatial relationship between antibody escape and receptor-binding fitness in viral evolution.
Main results
The results showed that in SARS-CoV-2, the receptor-binding interface overlaps with dominant antibody targets, restricting escape mutations to a narrow region and promoting periodic evolutionary leaps. In contrast, influenza A(H3N2) has a recessed pocket that decouples antibody pressure from receptor-binding fitness constraints.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that receptor geometry and protein surface topology strongly influence the accessible evolutionary trajectories of viruses, offering a novel structural framework for understanding antigenic evolution.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a mechanistic basis for predicting and monitoring antigenic evolution in seasonal influenza viruses, which can inform vaccine development and public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-level evidence, and the conclusions are based on integrated data from multiple sources, supporting a moderate confidence assessment.

C 补充信息学术文献 · 研究C supplementaryAcademic literature · Research

三体综合征21号染色体携带新型BACH2变异体的儿童出现播散性BCG疾病和反复肺部感染:一项5年随访病例报告

Disseminated BCG disease and recurrent pulmonary infections in a child with trisomy 21 carrying a novel BACH2 variant of uncertain significance: a 5-year case report
作者： Diana Marangu, Brian Maugo, Rita Afriye Boateng
摘要中文翻译背景:三体综合征21号染色体与联合免疫失调有关,包括淋巴细胞减少、对疫苗的反应受损以及对呼吸道感染的易感性增加,这在一定程度上是由21号染色体编码的干扰素受体基因三倍体引起的。BACH2单倍体不足是一种罕见的单基因免疫缺陷,表现为淋巴细胞减少、低丙种球蛋白血症以及对感染和自身免疫性疾病的易感性。这两种情况的共存尚未被描述过。病例报告:我们报告了一名肯尼亚女孩5年的随访过程,她患有三体综合征21号染色体、先天性心脏病、误吸和严重的胃食管反流病(GERD),通过全外显子组测序发现其携带一个意义未明的BACH2杂合错义变异体(c.1420C > G;p.Leu474Val);父母的遗传分离尚未完全明确。体外蛋白质结构建模(FoldX和Missense3D)对变异体对蛋白质稳定性和结构的影响预测不一致。其他体外病理性预测也存在不一致:Sorting Intolerant from Tolerant(SIFT)、Evolutionary Model of Variant Effect(EVE)、PolyPhen-2和CADD Phred评分预测该变异体具有致病性,而REVEL和AlphaMissense则认为其无致病性。在整个随访期间,均记录到持续的全淋巴细胞减少和显著降低的IgM水平。在5年期间,这名女孩经历了六次住院的下呼吸道感染(LRTIs),其中四次需要重症监护,包括播散性卡介苗(BCG)疾病、呼吸道合胞病毒和SARS-CoV-2感染,以及流感甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09肺炎。4岁时胸部计算机断层扫描显示双侧实变,但无支气管扩张。年度自身免疫筛查结果均为阴性。结论:该病例展示了三体综合征21号染色体和候选BACH2变异体的儿童所面临的严重且多样化的呼吸道病原体感染负担。三体综合征21号染色体本身可能解释了大部分表型;而BACH2变异体的独立贡献仍需功能研究和遗传分离研究来证实。本病例支持的临床考虑包括在预防BCG疾病前进行免疫学筛查的潜在价值、病毒预防药物的公平获取以及在难治性病例中早期专科介入的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
三体综合征21号染色体与联合免疫失调有关,包括淋巴细胞减少、对疫苗的反应受损以及对呼吸道感染的易感性增加,这在一定程度上是由21号染色体编码的干扰素受体基因三倍体引起的。
设计与对象
一项5年的随访描述了一名患有三体综合征21号染色体、先天性心脏病、误吸和严重胃食管反流病(GERD)的肯尼亚女孩,通过全外显子组测序发现其携带一个意义未明的BACH2杂合错义变异体(c.1420C > G;p.Leu474Val);父母的遗传分离尚未完全明确。
核心方法
本研究包括纵向临床观察、全外显子组测序以及体外蛋白质结构建模(FoldX和Missense3D),以评估该变异体对蛋白质稳定性和结构的影响。
主要结果
在5年期间,这名女孩经历了六次住院的下呼吸道感染(LRTIs),其中四次需要重症监护,包括播散性卡介苗(BCG)疾病、呼吸道合胞病毒和SARS-CoV-2感染,以及流感甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09肺炎。4岁时胸部计算机断层扫描显示双侧实变,但无支气管扩张。
解释与创新
该病例展示了三体综合征21号染色体和候选BACH2变异体的儿童所面临的严重且多样化的呼吸道病原体感染负担。这两种情况的共存尚未被描述过。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现突出了在预防BCG疾病前进行免疫学筛查的潜在价值、病毒预防药物的公平获取以及在难治性病例中早期专科介入的重要性。
局限与证据强度
由于缺乏功能研究和遗传分离研究来确认BACH2变异体的独立贡献,证据有限,且这些发现基于单一的5年随访。

Disseminated BCG disease and recurrent pulmonary infections in a child with trisomy 21 carrying a novel BACH2 variant of uncertain significance: a 5-year case report

Authors: Diana Marangu, Brian Maugo, Rita Afriye Boateng
Original abstract (English)
Background Trisomy 21 is associated with combined immune dysregulation, including lymphopenia, impaired responses to vaccines, and heightened susceptibility to respiratory infections, driven in part by triplication of chromosome 21-encoded interferon receptor genes. BACH2 haploinsufficiency is a rare monogenic immunodeficiency characterized by lymphopenia, hypogammaglobulinemia, and susceptibility to infections and autoimmune diseases. Co-occurrence of these two conditions has not been previously described. Case presentation We report a 5-year longitudinal course in a Kenyan girl with trisomy 21, congenital heart disease, aspiration, and severe gastroesophageal reflux disease (GERD) found via whole exome sequencing to carry a heterozygous BACH2 missense variant (c.1420C > G;p.Leu474Val) of uncertain significance; parental segregation remains incomplete. In silico protein structural modeling (FoldX and Missense3D) yielded discordant predictions of the variant's effect on the protein’s stability and structure. Additional in silico pathogenicity predictions were also inconsistent: Sorting Intolerant from Tolerant (SIFT), Evolutionary Model of Variant Effect (EVE), PolyPhen-2, and CADD Phred scores predicted pathogenicity, while REVEL and AlphaMissense suggested a lack of deleteriousness. Persistent panlymphopenia and profoundly low IgM levels were documented throughout the follow-up period. Over these 5 years, the girl sustained six hospitalized lower respiratory tract infections (LRTIs), with four requiring intensive care, which were disseminated Bacille Calmette-Guérin (BCG) disease, respiratory syncytial virus and SARS-CoV-2 infections, and influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 pneumonia. Chest computed tomography at 4 years showed bilateral consolidations without bronchiectasis. Annual autoimmune screening remained negative. Conclusion This case illustrates the burden caused by severe and diverse respiratory pathogenic infections in a child with trisomy 21 and a candidate BACH2 variant. Trisomy 21 alone plausibly explains much of the phenotype; an independent contribution from the BACH2 variant remains unproven pending functional and segregation studies. Clinical considerations supported by this case include the potential value of prevaccination immunological screening to prevent BCG disease, equitable access to viral prophylaxis, and early specialist engagement in refractory cases.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Trisomy 21 is associated with combined immune dysregulation, including lymphopenia, impaired responses to vaccines, and heightened susceptibility to respiratory infections, driven in part by triplication of chromosome 21-encoded interferon receptor genes.
Design and population
A 5-year case report describes a Kenyan girl with trisomy 21, congenital heart disease, aspiration, and severe gastroesophageal reflux disease (GERD) who was found via whole exome sequencing to carry a heterozygous BACH2 missense variant (c.1420C > G;p.Leu474Val) of uncertain significance; parental segregation remains incomplete.
Core methods
The study involved longitudinal clinical observation, whole exome sequencing, and in silico protein structural modeling (FoldX and Missense3D) to assess the variant's effect on the protein’s stability and structure.
Main results
The girl sustained six hospitalized lower respiratory tract infections (LRTIs) over 5 years, with four requiring intensive care, including disseminated BCG disease, respiratory syncytial virus and SARS-CoV-2 infections, and influenza A(H1N1)pdm09 pneumonia. Chest computed tomography at 4 years showed bilateral consolidations without bronchiectasis.
Interpretation and novelty
The case illustrates the burden caused by severe and diverse respiratory pathogenic infections in a child with trisomy 21 and a candidate BACH2 variant. The co-occurrence of these two conditions has not been previously described.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the potential value of prevaccination immunological screening to prevent BCG disease, equitable access to viral prophylaxis, and early specialist engagement in refractory cases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited by the lack of functional and segregation studies to confirm an independent contribution from the BACH2 variant, and the findings are based on a single case report.

季节性热变化动态及流感 发病率 在多个城市中的表现

Seasonal thermal transition dynamics and influenza incidence across multiple cities
作者： Yi-Han Wang, Zhi-Qiang Wang, Yue Cao, Yihan Wang, Zhiqiang Wang
摘要中文翻译本研究发现,快速的热变化和长期温度标准的偏离与多个城市中流感 发病率 的增加有关,这表明这些动态可能为预测流行病活动提供有价值的见解,超越传统的绝对温度测量。本研究探讨了热变化动态,除了绝对温度外,是否与流感 发病率 在不同气候背景下的关联。方法 我们对北京、东京、纽约、伦敦和悉尼进行了回顾性多城市分析。结果 描述性分析显示,报告的流感 发病率 增加经常与快速升温、延迟降温以及与城市特定温度基准的更大偏离同时发生。绝对温度异常幅度显示出不太一致的独立关联,而降雪发现仍处于探索阶段。由于分析是观察性的,并且涵盖了 COVID-19 大流行期间,这些发现应被解释为时间关联,而非因果效应。然而,将绝对温度水平与流感 发病率 相关联的实证发现,在不同地区和时间段之间存在不一致性。文章摘要 AI 生成的 季节性流感 暴发 在不同地区表现出显著的幅度和时间变化,突显了理解这些模式背后环境因素的必要性。摘要 背景 季节性流感 暴发 显示出强烈的时间规律性,但其幅度和时间在年份和地点之间有所不同。从 1965–2025 年的每日温度记录中构建了城市特定的气候基准,并与 2020–2025 年的每日流感 发病率 数据进行了比较。分析考虑了...
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在了解季节性热变化如何影响多个城市中流感 发病率 的表现,应对识别流感 暴发 模式中环境驱动因素变化的挑战。
设计与对象
本研究采用回顾性观察设计,将长期气候记录与短期流行病学时间序列相结合,聚焦于 1965–2025 年期间的北京、东京、纽约、伦敦和悉尼。流感病例与 COVID-19 病例分别分析,每日 发病率 使用 7 天移动平均进行平滑处理。
核心方法
每日流感 发病率 使用中心化的 7 天移动平均进行平滑处理,以减少高频噪声。通过皮尔逊相关分析,评估温度变化指标与流行病活动之间的关联,重点关注城市内部的时间共变性。相对湿度被作为补充的气候变量纳入分析,同样使用 7 天移动平均进行平滑处理,并分析其城市内部的时间共变性。
主要结果
描述性分析显示,报告的流感 发病率 增加经常与快速升温、延迟降温以及与城市特定温度基准的更大偏离同时发生。绝对温度异常幅度显示出不太一致的独立关联,而降雪发现仍处于探索阶段。
解释与创新
作者认为,热变化速率和与长期气候标准的偏离可能为描述流感活动时间提供补充信息,超越绝对温度单独的作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,热变化动态可能有助于改进 监测 和流感活动的预测模型,特别是在大流行影响的背景下,传统测量方法可能不够可靠。
局限与证据强度
本研究关注的是每个城市内部的相对时间变化,而非跨城市比较,且分析是在大流行影响的 监测 背景下进行的,这可能限制其普遍适用性。证据强度为中等。

Seasonal thermal transition dynamics and influenza incidence across multiple cities

Authors: Yi-Han Wang, Zhi-Qiang Wang, Yue Cao, Yihan Wang, Zhiqiang Wang
Original abstract (English)
This study found that rapid thermal transitions and deviations from long-term temperature norms are associated with increased influenza incidence in multiple cities, suggesting that these dynamics may provide valuable insights for predicting epidemic activity beyond traditional absolute temperature measures. This study examined whether thermal transition dynamics, in addition to absolute temperature, are associated with influenza incidence across contrasting climatic settings. Methods We conducted a retrospective multi-city analysis of Beijing, Tokyo, New York, London, and Sydney. Results Descriptive analyses showed that increases in reported influenza incidence frequently coincided with rapid warming, delayed cooling, and larger departures from city-specific temperature baselines. Absolute temperature-anomaly magnitude showed less consistent independent associations, and the snowfall findings remained exploratory. Because the analysis was observational and covered the COVID-19 pandemic period, the findings should be interpreted as temporal associations rather than causal effects. However, empirical findings linking absolute temperature levels to influenza incidence have been inconsistent across regions and time periods. Article summary AI generated Seasonal influenza outbreaks exhibit significant variability in magnitude and timing across different regions, highlighting the need to understand the environmental factors influencing these patterns. Abstract Background Seasonal influenza outbreaks show strong temporal regularity, but their magnitude and timing vary across years and locations. City-specific climatological baselines were constructed from daily temperature records for 1965–2025 and compared with daily influenza incidence data for 2020–2025. Analyses consider...
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to understand how seasonal thermal transitions influence influenza incidence across multiple cities, addressing the challenge of identifying environmental drivers of variability in outbreak patterns.
Design and population
The study employed a retrospective observational design integrating long-term climatological records with short-term epidemiological time series, focusing on Beijing, Tokyo, New York, London, and Sydney from 1965–2025. Influenza cases were analyzed separately from COVID-19 cases, with daily incidence smoothed using a 7-day moving average.
Core methods
Daily influenza incidence was smoothed using a centered 7-day moving average to reduce high-frequency noise. Associations between temperature transition metrics and epidemic activity were assessed descriptively using Pearson correlation analysis, focusing on within-city temporal co-variation. Relative humidity was included as a complementary climatic variable, also smoothed using a 7-day moving average, and analyzed for within-city temporal co-variation.
Main results
Descriptive analyses showed that increases in reported influenza incidence frequently coincided with rapid warming, delayed cooling, and larger departures from city-specific temperature baselines. Absolute temperature-anomaly magnitude showed less consistent independent associations, and the snowfall findings remained exploratory.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that thermal transition rate and deviation from long-term climatic norms may provide complementary information for describing the timing of influenza activity beyond absolute temperature alone.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that thermal transition dynamics could inform improved surveillance and prediction models for influenza activity, particularly in pandemic-affected contexts where traditional measures may be less reliable.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study focused on relative temporal variation within each city rather than cross-city comparisons, and analyses were conducted within the context of pandemic-affected surveillance, which may limit generalizability. The evidence is moderate.

西孟加拉邦一家三级医院急性呼吸道感染患者呼吸道病毒病原体的分子特征研究

A study on the molecular profiling of viral pathogens among patients with acute respiratory tract infections at a tertiary care hospital in West Bengal, India
作者： Malabika Biswas, Madhumanti Mandal, Anirban Bhaduri
摘要中文翻译背景:急性呼吸道感染是一种常见感染,其症状相互重叠。它是儿童疾病和发病率的主要原因之一。分子诊断方法,如实时逆转录聚合酶链反应(RT-PCR),仍然是区分此类感染的实用金标准。然而,印度关于呼吸道感染患者呼吸道病毒病原体分子特征的研究却十分匮乏。因此,本研究旨在进行呼吸道病原体的分子 监测。研究目的和目标:本研究旨在(i)描述急性呼吸道感染患者的流行病学和临床特征,(ii)确定通过RT-PCR检测到的呼吸道病毒病原体的分布情况。材料和方法:本研究是一项回顾性、医院为基础的观察性研究,时间从2025年7月至2026年7月。共有127名患者参与了本研究;所有样本均接受了多重实时RT-PCR检测。结果:39名(30.7%)患者至少检测到一种病毒。在研究人群中,甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09的比例最高,占11例(28.2%),其次是呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)占9例(23.1%),以及甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09以外的甲型流感病毒B型占7例(17.9%)。结论:在症状不明确且相互重叠的病毒性感染中,呼吸道病毒的分子特征尤为重要,对于合理的抗菌药物使用、感染控制措施和公共卫生准备具有重要意义。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在描述急性呼吸道感染患者的流行病学和临床特征,并确定通过RT-PCR检测到的呼吸道病毒病原体的分布情况,以弥补印度关于呼吸道感染患者病毒病原体分子特征研究的不足。
设计与对象
本研究是一项回顾性、医院为基础的观察性研究,时间从2025年7月至2026年7月,涉及127名急性呼吸道感染患者。
核心方法
所有样本均接受了多重实时RT-PCR检测以进行分子诊断。
主要结果
39名(30.7%)患者至少检测到一种病毒,其中甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09的比例最高(28.2%),其次是呼吸道合胞病毒(RSV)(23.1%)和甲型流感病毒B型(17.9%)。
解释与创新
本研究强调了分子特征在合理诊断和公共卫生准备中的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为呼吸道病毒性感染,特别是甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09的监测、诊断和治疗提供信息。
局限与证据强度
本研究的局限性在于其回顾性设计和样本量,这可能会影响其结果的普遍适用性。

A study on the molecular profiling of viral pathogens among patients with acute respiratory tract infections at a tertiary care hospital in West Bengal, India

Authors: Malabika Biswas, Madhumanti Mandal, Anirban Bhaduri
Original abstract (English)
Background: Acute respiratory tract infection is a frequently acquired infection characterized by overlapping symptoms. It is a major cause of childhood mortality and morbidity. Molecular diagnostics such as real-time reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR) remain the practical gold standard to distinguish between such infections. However, there is a dearth of studies across India on molecular profiling of viral pathogens among respiratory tract infection patients. Therefore, this study was conducted for molecular surveillance of respiratory pathogens. Aims and Objectives: The current study aimed to (i) describe the demographic and clinical profile of patients with acute respiratory tract infections, (ii) determine the distribution of respiratory viral pathogens detected by RT-PCR. Materials and Methods: This was a retrospective, hospital-based, and observational study conducted from July 2025 to July 2026. A total of 127 patients were enrolled in the study; all samples enrolled were subjected to multiplex real-time RT-PCR. Results: Thirty-nine (30.7%) patients were found to be positive for at least one type of virus. Influenza A (H1N1) pdm09 had the highest proportion in the study population, accounting for 11 (28.2%) of the cases, followed by respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) in 9 (23.1%) and Influenza B virus in 7 (17.9%) of the patients. Conclusion: Molecular profiling of respiratory viruses is especially important in the diagnosis of viral infections with non-distinct, overlapping symptoms and for proper antimicrobial stewardship, infection control practices and public health preparedness.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to describe the demographic and clinical profile of patients with acute respiratory tract infections and determine the distribution of respiratory viral pathogens detected by RT-PCR, addressing the dearth of studies on molecular profiling of viral pathogens among respiratory tract infection patients in India.
Design and population
A retrospective, hospital-based, and observational study was conducted from July 2025 to July 2026, involving 127 patients with acute respiratory tract infections.
Core methods
Samples were subjected to multiplex real-time RT-PCR for molecular diagnostics.
Main results
Thirty-nine (30.7%) patients were found to be positive for at least one type of virus, with Influenza A (H1N1) pdm09 having the highest proportion (28.2%), followed by respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) (23.1%) and Influenza B virus (17.9%).
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights the importance of molecular profiling for proper diagnosis and public health preparedness.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform surveillance, diagnosis, and treatment of respiratory viral infections, particularly Influenza A (H1N1) pdm09.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and sample size, which may affect generalizability.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 综述A high priorityAcademic literature · Review

33个美洲卫生组织成员国2026年家禽从业人员季节性流感 疫苗接种政策:一项综述

Seasonal Influenza Vaccination Policies for Poultry Workers in 33 Pan American Health Organization Member States in 2026: A Review
作者： José Luis Montes, Ximena Juárez, Susana Cristina Lloveras, Ana Paula Cordero, Carolina Saenz, Carolina Sáenz
摘要中文翻译背景/目的:自2004年以来,季节性流感 疫苗接种已被推荐作为家禽从业人员的公共卫生措施,以降低人类和禽流感病毒共感染的风险以及潜在的重组病毒株出现的可能性。拉丁美洲和加勒比地区(LAC)同时存在季节性流感和禽流感的共循环,暴发在不同动物物种中传播,包括人类。此外,LAC是主要的家禽生产地区,拥有大量接触家禽的劳动力,增加了病毒在人类与动物界面传播的机会。本综述旨在描述33个LAC国家当前的季节性流感 疫苗接种政策状况,并确定哪些国家明确将家禽从业人员列为目标群体。方法:我们通过从电子联合免疫报告表(eJRF)和33个国家的国家卫生部门网站进行结构化和迭代的文献检索策略,开展了文献综述。结果:截至2026年7月,有8个国家明确将家禽从业人员列为季节性流感 疫苗接种的目标群体:智利、哥斯达黎加、古巴、厄瓜多尔、危地马拉、洪都拉斯、秘鲁和乌拉圭。相比之下,有14个国家在其最近可在线获取的疫苗接种政策中未提及家禽从业人员。对于另外11个国家,未找到公开的可用数据。结论:在LAC地区,家禽从业人员季节性流感 疫苗接种政策的采纳和报告存在显著差距。大多数该地区的主要家禽生产国并未特别针对这一群体,也未在记录禽流感暴发的国家中特别提及。这代表了在人类与动物界面加强预防措施的错失机会。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
对33个美洲卫生组织成员国家禽从业人员季节性流感 疫苗接种政策进行综述,以评估这些政策在多大程度上明确将家禽从业人员列为目标群体。
证据基础与方法
通过从电子联合免疫报告表(eJRF)和33个国家的国家卫生部门网站进行结构化和迭代的文献检索策略,开展了文献综述。
共识与结论
有8个国家明确将家禽从业人员列为季节性流感 疫苗接种的目标群体:智利、哥斯达黎加、古巴、厄瓜多尔、危地马拉、洪都拉斯、秘鲁和乌拉圭。相比之下,有14个国家在其最近可在线获取的疫苗接种政策中未提及家禽从业人员。对于另外11个国家,未找到公开的可用数据。
争议与缺口
在LAC地区,家禽从业人员季节性流感 疫苗接种政策的采纳和报告存在显著差距。大多数该地区的主要家禽生产国并未特别针对这一群体,也未在记录禽流感暴发的国家中特别提及。
科研与实践启示
这代表了在人类与动物界面加强预防措施的错失机会。需要改进针对家禽从业人员的监测、记录和实施疫苗接种政策,以减轻共感染和重组病毒株出现的风险。

Seasonal Influenza Vaccination Policies for Poultry Workers in 33 Pan American Health Organization Member States in 2026: A Review

Authors: José Luis Montes, Ximena Juárez, Susana Cristina Lloveras, Ana Paula Cordero, Carolina Saenz, Carolina Sáenz
Original abstract (English)
Background/Objective: Since 2004, seasonal influenza vaccination has been technically recommended for poultry workers as a public health measure to reduce the risk of co-infection with human and avian influenza viruses and the potential emergence of reassortant strains. The Latin America and the Caribbean (LAC) region has co-circulation of seasonal and avian influenza, with outbreaks in different animal species, including humans. Also, LAC is a major poultry-producing region, with large workforces exposed to domestic poultry, increasing opportunities for virus transmission at the human–animal interface. This review aimed to describe the current state of seasonal influenza vaccination policies across 33 LAC countries and to identify which countries explicitly mention poultry workers. Methods: We conducted a literature review with structured and iterative search strategies from the electronic Joint Reporting Form on Immunization (eJRF) and the national health authorities’ websites of the 33 countries. Results: As of July 2026, eight countries explicitly included poultry workers among the target groups for seasonal influenza vaccination: Chile, Costa Rica, Cuba, Ecuador, Guatemala, Honduras, Peru and Uruguay. In contrast, 14 countries did not mention poultry workers in their most recent vaccination policies available online. For 11 countries, no publicly available data was identified. Conclusions: Substantial gaps exist in the adoption and reporting of seasonal influenza vaccination policies for poultry workers across the LAC region. Most of the region’s major poultry-producing countries do not specifically target this group, nor do most of the countries where outbreaks of avian influenza were recorded. This represents a missed opportunity to strengthen prevention efforts at the human–animal interface.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
Seasonal influenza vaccination policies for poultry workers in 33 Pan American Health Organization member states were reviewed to assess the extent to which these policies explicitly include poultry workers as target groups.
Evidence base and method
A literature review was conducted using structured and iterative search strategies from the electronic Joint Reporting Form on Immunization (eJRF) and national health authorities’ websites of the 33 countries.
Consensus and conclusions
Eight countries explicitly included poultry workers among the target groups for seasonal influenza vaccination: Chile, Costa Rica, Cuba, Ecuador, Guatemala, Honduras, Peru, and Uruguay. In contrast, 14 countries did not mention poultry workers in their most recent vaccination policies available online. For 11 countries, no publicly available data was identified.
Controversies and gaps
Substantial gaps exist in the adoption and reporting of seasonal influenza vaccination policies for poultry workers across the LAC region. Most of the region’s major poultry-producing countries do not specifically target this group, nor do most of the countries where outbreaks of avian influenza were recorded.
Research and practice implications
This represents a missed opportunity to strengthen prevention efforts at the human–animal interface. There is a need for improved surveillance, documentation, and implementation of vaccination policies targeting poultry workers to mitigate the risk of co-infection and reassortant strain emergence.

流感A病毒感染中双抗病毒药物和植物化学物的免疫调节作用:靶向关键宿主信号通路。

Dual Antiviral and Immunomodulatory Effects of Phytochemicals in Influenza A Virus Infection: Targeting Key Host Signaling Pathways.
作者： Chowdhury D, Vanderven HA, Wangchuk P, Sarker S
摘要中文翻译流感A病毒(IAV)由于其高度的遗传变异性和逃避宿主 免疫应答的能力,仍然是一个重要的公共卫生威胁。尽管抗病毒药物药物和疫苗仍是应对流感感染的关键措施,但其有限的疗效以及耐药病毒株的出现促使人们日益关注探索互补的治疗方案。在本综述中,我们批判性地评估了当前关于植物来源化合物如何调节先天免疫信号网络的证据,包括在IAV感染期间的Toll样受体、视黄酸诱导基因I、丝裂原活化蛋白激酶和核因子κB通路。我们进一步探讨了支撑这种广泛免疫调节活性的通路交叉对话,以及目前限制其临床进展的翻译障碍,包括生物利用度、代谢转化和标准化问题,同时指出了未来研究必须解决的机制性空白。总体而言,本综述认为植物化学物应被理解为整合先天免疫网络的调节因子,这一观点重新定义了它们的治疗潜力以及推进其应用所需的实验标准。
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
本综述聚焦于季节性流感病毒,特别是人类流感的甲型H1N1流感病毒pdm09、甲型H3N2流感病毒、B/Victoria和B/Yamagata谱系。核心问题是植物来源化合物如何在流感A病毒(IAV)感染期间调节先天免疫信号网络,重点包括Toll样受体、视黄酸诱导基因I、丝裂原活化蛋白激酶和核因子κB通路。
证据基础与方法
本综述是对当前关于植物化学物在IAV感染期间免疫调节作用的证据进行批判性评估。它包括对抗病毒药物药物和疫苗局限性的总体概述,综述的范围,以及植物来源化合物靶向的通路。综述未明确报告元分析或汇总估计,但概述了评估证据所使用的一般方法。
共识与结论
综述得出结论,植物化学物应被理解为整合先天免疫网络的调节因子,这一观点重新定义了它们的治疗潜力以及推进其应用所需的实验标准。它强调了在IAV感染期间关键宿主信号通路的调节。
争议与缺口
综述指出了限制植物化学物临床进展的翻译障碍,如生物利用度、代谢转化和标准化问题。它还指出未来研究必须解决的机制性空白,以更好地理解这些化合物的免疫调节活性。
科研与实践启示
综述建议未来研究应专注于解决机制性空白和应对限制植物化学物临床应用的翻译障碍。它还强调了在IAV感染背景下评估其疗效和安全性的标准化方法的重要性。}}.{

Dual Antiviral and Immunomodulatory Effects of Phytochemicals in Influenza A Virus Infection: Targeting Key Host Signaling Pathways.

Authors: Chowdhury D, Vanderven HA, Wangchuk P, Sarker S
Original abstract (English)
Influenza A virus (IAV) remains a major public health threat due to its high genetic variability and ability to evade host immune responses. While antiviral drugs and vaccines remain the key measures against influenza infection, their limited efficacy and the emergence of resistant viral strains have prompted growing interest in exploring complementary therapeutic approaches. In this review, we critically evaluate current evidence on how plant-derived compounds modulate innate immune signaling networks, including toll-like receptor, retinoic acid-inducible gene I, mitogen-activated protein kinase, and nuclear factor kappa B pathways during IAV infection. We further address the pathway cross-talk that underpins this broad immunomodulatory activity, the translational barriers including bioavailability, metabolic transformation, and standardization that currently limit clinical progression, and the mechanistic gaps that future studies must resolve. Collectively, this review argues that phytocompounds should be understood as modulators of an integrated innate immune network, an insight that reframes both their therapeutic potential and the experimental standards required to advance them.
View review five-element analysis
Scope and question
The review focuses on seasonal influenza viruses, specifically human influenza A(H1N1)pdm09, A(H3N2), and B/Victoria and B/Yamagata lineages. The central question is how plant-derived compounds modulate innate immune signaling networks during influenza A virus (IAV) infection, with an emphasis on toll-like receptor, retinoic acid-inducible gene I, mitogen-activated protein kinase, and nuclear factor kappa B pathways.
Evidence base and method
The review is a critical evaluation of current evidence on the immunomodulatory effects of phytochemicals during IAV infection. It includes a general overview of the limitations of antiviral drugs and vaccines, the scope of the review, and the pathways targeted by plant-derived compounds. The review does not explicitly report on meta-analysis or pooled estimates, but it outlines the general methodology used to assess the evidence.
Consensus and conclusions
The review concludes that phytochemicals should be understood as modulators of an integrated innate immune network, which reframes their therapeutic potential and the experimental standards required for their advancement. It highlights the modulation of key host signaling pathways during IAV infection.
Controversies and gaps
The review identifies translational barriers such as bioavailability, metabolic transformation, and standardization that limit the clinical progression of phytochemicals. It also notes mechanistic gaps that future studies must resolve to better understand the immunomodulatory activity of these compounds.
Research and practice implications
The review suggests that future research should focus on resolving the mechanistic gaps and addressing the translational barriers that hinder the clinical application of phytochemicals. It also emphasizes the need for standardized approaches to evaluate their efficacy and safety in the context of IAV infection.

RNA基流感疫苗疫苗:前沿综述

Navigating the Transition to RNA-Based Influenza Vaccines: A State-of-the-Art Review
作者： Antonios-Periklis Panagiotopoulos, Deny Tsakri, Alexandros Laskaris, Chrysa Saldari, Kyriaki Ranellou, Ilias Mariolis, Athanasios Tsakris, Cleo G. Anastassopoulou
摘要中文翻译
查看综述五要素
范围与问题
季节性流感以及包括高致病性禽流感A(H5N1)在内的兽源性流感病毒的持续出现仍然是全球公共卫生的重大威胁,凸显了对快速适应的疫苗平台的迫切需求。
证据基础与方法
本前沿综述通过全面评估ClinicalTrials.gov记录和同行评审文献,对RNA基流感疫苗的临床开发进行了批判性评价。候选疫苗被分为四个领域进行考察:季节性流感 疫苗、流行前流感疫苗、组合呼吸道疫苗以及自扩增RNA(saRNA)平台。saRNA流感疫苗的临床开发已进入早期阶段试验,但公开的临床数据仍然有限。
共识与结论
临床证据表明,mRNA流感疫苗能够持续引发强效的体液免疫应答,并且在后期试验中显示出相较于传统流感疫苗对流感A病毒更高的相对有效性。流行前和多组分mRNA 疫苗候选疫苗也显示出强大的免疫原性,支持RNA平台在季节性流感疫苗接种和大流行应对中的潜力。
争议与缺口
mRNA 疫苗通常表现出更高的反应性,尤其是在短暂的轻度至中度全身不良事件方面。然而,严重不良事件仍然较为罕见,并且与已获批的比较疫苗所观察到的不良事件大致相当。
科研与实践启示
Approval of mRNA-1010 (mFlusiva), the first approved mRNA-based seasonal influenza vaccine, and European Union authorization of mRNA-1083 (mCOMBRIAX), an mRNA-based influenza-COVID-19 combination vaccine, represent important steps in the clinical transition and implementation of RNA-based influenza vaccination.

Navigating the Transition to RNA-Based Influenza Vaccines: A State-of-the-Art Review

Authors: Antonios-Periklis Panagiotopoulos, Deny Tsakri, Alexandros Laskaris, Chrysa Saldari, Kyriaki Ranellou, Ilias Mariolis, Athanasios Tsakris, Cleo G. Anastassopoulou
Original abstract (English)
Seasonal influenza and the ongoing emergence of zoonotic influenza viruses, including highly pathogenic avian influenza A (H5N1), remain substantial global public health threats, underscoring the need for rapidly adaptable vaccine platforms. This state-of-the-art review critically evaluates the clinical development of RNA-based influenza vaccines through a comprehensive assessment of ClinicalTrials.gov records and peer-reviewed literature. Candidates were examined across four domains: seasonal influenza vaccines, pre-pandemic influenza vaccines, combination respiratory vaccines, and self-amplifying RNA (saRNA) platforms. Clinical evidence indicates that mRNA influenza vaccines consistently elicit robust humoral immune responses and, in late-stage trials, have demonstrated improved relative efficacy against influenza A compared with conventional influenza vaccines. Reactogenicity is generally more frequent with mRNA vaccines, particularly with respect to transient mild-to-moderate systemic adverse events. However, serious adverse events remain uncommon and broadly comparable to those observed with licensed comparator vaccines. Pre-pandemic and multicomponent mRNA vaccine candidates similarly demonstrate strong immunogenicity, supporting the potential of RNA platforms for both seasonal vaccination and pandemic preparedness. Clinical development of saRNA influenza vaccines has progressed through early-phase trials, although publicly available clinical data remain limited. Regulatory milestones in 2026, including U.S. approval of mRNA-1010 (mFlusiva), the first approved mRNA-based seasonal influenza vaccine, and European Union authorization of mRNA-1083 (mCOMBRIAX), an mRNA-based influenza-COVID-19 combination vaccine, represent important steps in the clinical transition and implementation of RNA-based influenza vaccination.

Clinical development of saRNA influenza vaccines has progressed through early-phase trials, although publicly available clinical data remain limited.
Consensus and conclusions
Clinical evidence indicates that mRNA influenza vaccines consistently elicit robust humoral immune responses and, in late-stage trials, have demonstrated improved relative efficacy against influenza A compared with conventional influenza vaccines. Pre-pandemic and multicomponent mRNA vaccine candidates similarly demonstrate strong immunogenicity, supporting the potential of RNA platforms for both seasonal vaccination and pandemic preparedness.
Controversies and gaps
Reactogenicity is generally more frequent with mRNA vaccines, particularly with respect to transient mild-to-moderate systemic adverse events. However, serious adverse events remain uncommon and broadly comparable to those observed with licensed comparator vaccines.
Research and practice implications
Approval of mRNA-1010 (mFlusiva), the first approved mRNA-based seasonal influenza vaccine, and European Union authorization of mRNA-1083 (mCOMBRIAX), an mRNA-based influenza-COVID-19 combination vaccine, represent important steps in the clinical transition and implementation of RNA-based influenza vaccination.
